# Siddhānta-ratnam \[Bhanu Swami\]

## siddhānta-ratnam \[Bhanu Svami\]

Gem of Conclusions

## Chapter One

### Text 1

*Defining the means to the ultimate human goal*  
*vedās tathā smṛti-giro yam acintya-śaktiṁ*  
*sṛṣṭi-sthiti-pralaya-kāraṇam āmananti \|*  
*taṁ śyāmasundaram avikriyam ātma-mūrtiṁ*  
*sarveśvaraṁ praṇati-mātra-vaśaṁ bhajāmaḥ \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I worship unchanging Śyāmasundara, the Lord of all, the very form of ātmā (knowledge and bliss), who is controlled by offerings of respects and other forms of bhakti, and who is called the cause of creation, maintenance and destruction by the Vedas and smṛṭis, through his acintya-śakti. \|\|1\|\|**

### Text 2

*gajapatir anukampā-sampadā yasya sadyaḥ*  
*samajani niravadyaḥ sāndram ānandam ṛcchan \|*  
*nivasatu mama tasmin kṛṣṇacaitanya-rūpe*  
*matir atimadhurimṇā dīpyamāne murārau \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**May my mind dwell upon the form of Kṛṣṇa-caitanya, who is none other than Kṛṣṇa, who shines with the highest sweetness, and by whose mercy Pratāparudra, attaining intense bliss, suddenly became excellent (giving up his identity as a king). \|\|2\|\|**

### Text 3

*devābhyarthana-mandareṇa mathitād bhaktīndirā’bhūd yataḥ*  
*śrīmad-bhāgavatākhya-nirjara-tarūḥ sat-sūtra-ratnotkaraḥ \|*  
*dīvyad-gīti-sudhāṁśukāmṛta-rucir jñānaṁ ca dhanvantariḥ*  
*sa śrī-vyāsa-mahāmbudhir vijayate prītyai samantāt satām \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The great ocean of Vyāsa remains victorious for pleasing the devotees completely, since, from churning the Mandara mountain in the form of the devatās’ request for help, he produced Lakṣmī in the form of bhakti, he produced the immortal tree called Bhāgavatam, he produced the gem of Vedānta-sūtras, he produced the taste of nectar from the moon of attractive kīrtana, and he produced Dhanvantari in the form of knowledge (distinguishing matter, jīva and īśvara). \|\|3\|\|**

### Text 4

*govindābhidham indirāśrita-padaṁ hasta-stha-ratnādivat*  
*tattvaṁ tattvavid-uttamau kṣiti-tale yau darśayāñcakratuḥ \|*  
*māyāvāda-mahāndhakāra-paṭalī-sat-puṣpavantau sadā*  
*tau śrī-rūpa-sanātanau viracitāścaryau suvaryau stumaḥ \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I continually praise the excellent, astonishing Rūpa and Sanātana, the best knowers of tattva, who revealed on earth the truth (tattva), Govinda, whose feet are served by Lakṣmī, as if he were a jewel in one’s hand. I praise Rūpa and Sanātana, who are like the sun and moon for extinguishing the covering of great darkness of Mayāvāda philosophy. \|\|4\|\|**

### Text 5

*iha hi sukha-duḥkha-prāpti-parihārayor loka-pravṛttir dṛśyate \| tau copeya-bhūtāv upāyam antarā na sambhavetām \| ataḥ sārāsāra-vicāra-jñā maharṣayas tatropāyaṁ prakīrtayanti \| tatra prakṛti-puruṣāvivekād asya trividha-duḥkhotpādaḥ tad-vivekāt punar anādy-aviveka-nivṛttau puruṣaṁ prati nivṛttādhikārā prakṛtir bhavatīti tasya trividhasya duḥkhasya pradhvaṁsaḥ syāt \| sa ca kāryo’pi nityaḥ, abhāva-rūpatvāt \| sa evānandāvāptir ity upacaritaḥ bhārāpagame sukhī saṁvṛtta itivat, na tu tasmāt sātiricyat iti kapilaḥ \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In this world, people are engaged in seeking happiness and avoiding suffering. But the aims of removing suffering and attaining happiness cannot produce anything except various methods. Thus, great sages (like Kapila and Gautama), knowledgeable of distinguishing essence from non-essence, glorify their own methods. \|\|5\|\|**

**Комментарий:**

(Kapila’s philosophy)

Three types of misery arise from not distinguishing prakṛṭi from puruṣa, the. From distinguishing prakṛṭi from puruṣa, the beginningless non-distinction (ignorance) is destroyed. Then prakṛṭi has no control over puruṣa and the three types of misery will be destroyed. This result is eternal, since it is the form of non-existence (abhāva) of ignorance. (Abhāva is eternal). Attainment of bliss accompanies this, just as one becomes happy on getting rid of a heavy weight. Nothing can surpass this state. This is Kapila’s opinion.

### Text 6

*prakṛti-puruṣa-vivekābhyāsa-vairāgya-paripākād yama-niyamāsana-prāṇāyāma-pratyāhāra-dhāraṇa-dhyāna-saṁprajñāta-samādhi-pūrvakāt parameśvara-prasādajāt pañca-vidha-citta-vṛtti-nirodhād eva dharma-megha-śabda-vācyād asaṁprajñāta-samādher asya tāv iti patañjaliḥ \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Patañjali’s theory:**  
**Patañjali says that by the practice of distinguishing prakṛṭi from puruṣa, one develops renunciation. When this matures, one practices yama, niyama, āsana, prāṇāyāma, pratyāhāra, dhāraṇa, dhyāna and samprajñāta-samādhi. This pleases the supreme Lord.**

### Text 7

*dehendriyādi-vilakṣaṇo vibhur ayam ātmā nava-viśeṣa-guṇāśrayas tasya dravya-guṇa-karma-sāmānya-viśeṣa-samavāyānāṁ sādharmya-vaidharmyābhyāṁ tattva-jñānena sākṣātkārād īśvaropāsanā-sahitān navānāṁ vaiśeṣika-guṇānāṁ prāg-abhāvāsaha-vṛttir dhvaṁso bhavet, sa evānandāvāptir iti kaṇādaḥ \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Kanāda’s theory:**  
**The ātmā is called vibhu (all pervading) and is different from the body and senses. The ātmā takes shelter of nine special qualities (intelligence, happiness, suffering, desire, hatred, effort, dharma, adharma and conception or bhāvana). By consideration of similarity and difference of dravya, guṇa, karma, sāmānya, viśeṣa and samavāya, the ātmā understands the truth. By worship of the Lord,**

### Text 8

*pramāṇa-prameyādi-ṣoḍaśa-padārthānām uddeśa-lakṣaṇa-parīkṣābhir ātmādi-dvādaśa-vidha-prameya-niṣkarṣeṇātma-sākṣātkārāt śravaṇa-manana-nididhyāsana-pūrvakāt sa-vāsana-mithyā-jñāna-nivṛttau tat-kāryāṇāṁ rāga-dveṣa-mohānāṁ nivṛttis tatas tat-kāryayoḥ pravṛtti-pūrvakayor dharmādharmayos tataḥ pūrvārjita-karmaṇāṁ kāya-vyūha-pūrvakaṁ bhogena parikṣayād dehāntarānārambhas tato bādhanā-lakṣaṇasyaikaviṁśati-vidhasya duḥkhasyātyantikī nivṛttir bhavet, saiva sukhāvāptir iti gautamaḥ \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Gautama’s theory**  
**By enumeration, definition and examination of the sixteen categories, one realizes the ātmā by distinguishing twelve prameyas (ātmā, body, senses, sense objects, intelligence, mind, action, fault, transmigration, results, suffering and liberation.) Then, by hearing, contemplation and meditation, false knowledge and impressions are destroyed. From this arises destruction of illusion, attraction and hatred along with their effects. Then their effects- action, dharma and adharma--are destroyed. The previously accrued karmas are then destroyed by enjoyment. One does not take another body. Twenty-one types of suffering are destroyed. One attains happiness. This is Gautama’s theory. \|\|8\|\|**

### Text 9

*vedoktaiḥ śubha-karmabhir duḥkha-hāniḥ sukha-lābhaś ceti jaiminiḥ \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Jaimini’s theory:**  
**One obtains destruction of suffering by performing auspicious actions described in the Vedas. One then attains happiness. That is Jaimini’s theory. \|\|9\|\|**

### Text 10

*sarve hy ete upāyās tayor ātyantikayoḥ siddhāye nāṅgīkāryāḥ paramācāryeṇa bhagavatā śrī-bādarāyaṇena tān-matānāṁ nirākṛtatvāt \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**All these methods are certainly not be accepted for achieving final destruction of suffering and attainment of happiness, since Vyāsa, the supreme ācārya, has refuted these theories. \|\|10\|\|**

### Text 11

*kintu sarveśvarābhikhyasya puruṣottamasya svarūpato guṇataś ca parijñānaṁ sva-jñāna-pūrvakaṁ tasyai kalpate \| tathā hi— “jñātvā devaṁ sarva-pāśāpahāniḥ \| kṣīṇaiḥ kleśaiḥ janma-mṛtyu-prahāniḥ \| tasyābhidhyānāt tṛtīyaṁ deha-bhede viśvaiśvaryaṁ kevalam āpta-kāmaḥ \|\|” “jñātvā devaṁ sarva-pāśāpahāniḥ \| kṣīṇaiḥ kleśaiḥ janma-mṛtyu-prahāniḥ \| tasyābhidhyānāt tṛtīyaṁ deha-bhede \| viśvaiśvaryaṁ kevalam āpta-kāmaḥ \|\|” \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**However, complete knowledge of the supreme person or Lord of all beings, from knowing the Lord’s svarūpa and his qualities, along with knowledge of ātmā, achieves complete destruction of suffering and attainment of happiness.**

**Having understood the Lord, one is free of all bondage, suffering, birth and death. By meditation on the Lord, on leaving the body, one goes to the third place filled with the Lord's powers and without māyā's influence, and is fully satisfied. Śvetāśvatara Upaniṣad 1.11**

**When the** ***yogī,*** **after knowing about the unborn, unchanging, pure Lord with all His ornaments, realizes that supreme Brahman through the realization of the** ***ātmā*** **which is like a lamp, he attains liberation from all bondage.** ***Śvetāśvatara Upaniṣad*** **2.15 \|\|11\|\|**

### Text 12

*nanu, kiṁ tasya puruṣottamasya svarūpaṁ ke tasya guṇāḥ kīdṛśāś ca te yad vijñānād vimuktir iti cet, ucyate—vijñānānandas tasya svarūpaṁ, “vijñānam ānandaṁ brahma rātir dātuḥ parāyaṇam” \[bṛ.ā.u. 3.9.28\], “ānando brahmeti vyajānāt” \[tai.u. 3.5\], “raso vai saḥ” \[tai.u. 2.7\] ity-ādi-śravaṇāt \| tad eva vigraha-rūpam iti mantavyam \| na tu svarūpād vigrahasyātirekaḥ \| “yad-ātmako bhagavāṁs tad-ātmikā vyaktiḥ \| kim-ātmako bhagavān? jñānātmaka aiśvaryātmakaḥ śākty-ātmakaś” ceti \| “buddhi-mano’ṅga-pratyaṅgavattām bhagavato lakṣyāmahe buddhimān manovān aṅga-pratyaṅgavān” iti \| “tam ekaṁ govindaṁ saccidānanda-vigraham” \[go.tā.u. 1\] iti, “ardha-mātrātmako rāmo brahmānandaika-vigrahaḥ” iti caivam-ādi-śravaṇāt, “vedānte prathitaṁ tejaḥ” ity-ādi-smṛteś ca \| itthaṁ ca satyam eva jaḍaṁ bhagavad-rūpam iti jñānam evācetanaṁ tad iti ca pratyuktam \| tad evaṁ bhagavad-vigrahasyaiva brahmatvāt tasya vaiṣṇavādi-vākyād yad anyathā pratyayanaṁ tat tv āsurān prati māyayaiva— “ rājan parasya tanu-bhṛj-jananāpyayehā māyā-viḍambanam avehi yathā naṭasya \|” ity-ādikād ekādaśādi-vākyād eva \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“What is the svarūpa of the supreme Lord and what are his qualities? What is their nature such that, by knowing them, one attains liberation?”**

**His svarūpa is knowledge and bliss. Śruti says: vijñānam ānandaṁ brahma rātir dātuḥ parāyaṇam**  
**The Lord is knowledge and bliss, the giver, the goal of the sacrificer. (Bṛhad-āraṇyaka Upaniṣad 3.9.28) ānando brahmeti vyajānāt**  
**He understood that Brahman is bliss. (Taittirīya Upaniṣad 3.2) raso vai saḥ**  
**The Lord is rasa. Taittirīya Upaniṣad**

**One should understand that this indicates his form is his body-- not that his body is different from his form.**

***yad ātmako bhagavāṁs tad-ātmikā vyaktiḥ kim ātmako bhagavān jñānātmaka aiśvaryātmakaḥ śaktyātmakaḥ***

**What constitutes the Lord becomes manifest as non-different from himself. What constitutes the Lord? The Lord’s very essence is knowledge, power and specific śaktis. (quoted by Madhva)**

***buddhi-mano’ṅga-pratyaṅgavattāṁ bhagavato lakṣayāmahe,  buddhimān manovān aṅga-pratyaṅgavān***

**We describe the Lord endowed with limbs, subsections, mind and intelligence. He has intelligence, mind, limbs and subsections. (quoted in Paramātmā Sandarbha) tam ekaṁ govindaṁ saccidānanda-vigraham**

**Along with the Maruts, I eternally satisfy Govinda, a form of eternity, knowledge and bliss, non-different from his mantra, seated under a desire tree in Vṛndāvana, by offering praise. Gopāla-tāpanī Upaniṣad ardha-mātrātmako rāmo brahmānandaika-vigrahaḥ**

**Rāma, consisting of half a syllable, is the form of the bliss of Brahman. Rāma-tāpanī Upaniṣad vedānte prathitaṁ tejaḥ**  
**In the Upaniṣads, the Lord is called tejaḥ: he is power. Hari-vaṁśa**  
**This is the truth, and answers the claims that the Lord’s form is material, and that knowledge is some unconscious product. The Lord has a form which is Brahman, but māyā gives the demons another belief, which is different from what is actually stated in Viṣṇu Purāṇa.**

**rājan parasya tanu-bhṛj-jananāpyayehā**  
**māyā-viḍambanam avehi yathā naṭasya**

**O King! You should understand that the Supreme Lord’s appearance and disappearance, which resemble those of embodied conditioned souls, are actually a show enacted by his illusory energy, just like the performance of an actor. SB 11.31.11 \|\|12\|\|**

### Text 13

*jñānānanda-vigrahatā tu tasya śruti-mātreṇa gamyā na tu tarkaiḥ, acintyālaukika-vastutvāt \| tad idam abhihitaṁ bhagavatā sūtra-kāreṇa, arūpavad eva hi tat-pradhānatvāt \[bra. sū.3.2.14\], prakāśavac cāvaiyārthyam \[bra.sū. 3.2.15\], āha ca tanmātram \[bra.sū. 3.2.16\], darśayati cātho api smaryate \[bra.sū. 3.2.17\] ity asminn adhikaraṇe tathaiva bhāṣitaṁ ca \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**That the Lord has a form of knowledge and bliss is understood only by śruti and not by argumentation, since the Lord is inconceivable and beyond the material world. This is indicated by the Lord, author of the Vedānta-sūtras.**

**arūpavad eva hi tat-pradhānatvāt**

**The Lord does not possess a form since his form is his very self. Vedānta-sūtra 3.2.14**

**prakāśavac cāvaiyārthyam**

**The form of Brahman is not useless, as in the case of the sun. Vedānta-sūtra 3.2.15**

**āha ca tanmātram**

**And the scriptures say that the form is the Lord. Vedānta-sūtra 3.2.16**

**darśayati cātho api smaryate**

**Śruti shows the Lord and his form are non-different. Smṛti confirms this. Vedānta-sūtra 3.2.17 \|\|13\|\|**

### Text 14

*paramātma-jijñāsos tu tarko’nupādeya eva, “naiṣā tarkeṇa matir āpaneyā proktānyenaiva sujñānāya preṣṭha” \[ka.u. 1.2.9\], “acintyāḥ khalu ye bhāvā na tāṁs tarkeṇa yojayet” \[ma. bhā. 6.6.11\] ity ādi-śruti-smṛtibhyaḥ \| nanu mantavya iti tarkābhyupagamaḥ \| satyam \| vedāntārthānuguṇo’sau na nivāryate \| “pūrvottarāvirodhena ko’trārtho’bhimato bhavet ity-ādyam ūhanaṁ tarkaḥ \| śuṣka-tarkaṁ tu varjayed” ity-ādi-smṛteḥ \| tad evaṁ vijñānānanda-vigrahānatireki tasya svarūpam iti siddham \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Logic is unsuitable for inquiry about Paramātmā.**  
**naiṣā tarkeṇa matir āpaneyā proktānyenaiva sujñānāya preṣṭha**  
**This knowledge cannot be achieved by logic. O dear one! It should be taught by another person in order to be understood properly. Kaṭha Upaniṣad 1.2.9**  
**acintyāḥ khalu ye bhāvā na tāṁs tarkeṇa yojayet**  
**Inconceivable states are not subject to logic. (Mahābhārata)**  
**“But the word mantavyaḥ (contemplating) is used in describing Brahman. This indicates that logic is admitted.” That is true. Logic which follows the meaning of Vedānta is not rejected.**  
**Thus, it is established that the svarūpa of the Lord is non-different from knowledge and bliss. \|\|14\|\|**

### Text 15

*guṇāś ca satya-kāmatva-satya-saṅkalpatvādayo jñānānandādayaś cānantāḥ, te’pi svarūpān nātiricyante, eṣa ātmā’pahata-pāpmā vijaro vimṛtyur viśoko vijighatso’pipāsaḥ satya-kāmaḥ satya-saṅkalpa \[chā.u. 8.1.5\] iti, yaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarva-viprasya jñāna-mayaṁ tapa \[mu.u. 1.1.9, 2.2.7\] iti, ānandaṁ brahmaṇo vidvān \[tai.u. 2.4\] iti caivam-ādi-śravaṇāt \| “yathodakaṁ durge vṛṣṭaṁ parvateṣu vidhāvati \| evaṁ dharmān pṛthak paśyaṁs tān evānuvidhāvati \|\| “\[ka.u. 2.1.14\] “brahmaṇas tad-guṇānāṁ ca bheda-darśy adhamaṁ tamaḥ \| bhedābheda-pradarśī‚ tu madhyamaṁ tu tamo vrajet \|\|” ity-ādiṣu guṇātireka-darśino vigarhitatva-śravaṇāt \| ata eva “neha nānāsti kiñcana” iti tad anyat pratiṣiddham \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord’s qualities, such as satya-kāma (lover of truth), satya-saṅkalpa (whose will is fulfilled), knowledge and bliss are unlimited. They are non-different from his svarūpa.**  
**eṣa ātmā’pahata-pāpmā vijaro vimṛtyur viśoko vijighatso’pipāsaḥ satya-kāmaḥ satya-saṅkalpa**  
**One should know that** ***ātmā*** **is without sin, without ageing, without death, without lamentation, without hunger, without thirst, whose every desire is truth and whose every desire is fulfilled.** ***Chāndogya Upaniṣad*** **8.1.5**  
**yaḥ sarva-jñaḥ sarva-vid yasya jñāna-mayaṁ tapaḥ. tasmād etad brahma nāma-rūpam annaṁ ca jāyate**  
**From Brahman, who is omniscient, who knows everything, whose austerity is filled with knowledge, arose Brahman as food, which has name and form.** ***Muṇḍaka Upaniṣad*** **(1.1.9)**  
**ānandaṁ brahmaṇo vidvān** ***na bibheti kutaścana***  
**Knowing Brahman as bliss, one fears nothing. (Taittirīya Upaniṣad 2.4.1)**  
**As water falling on mountains flows to inaccessible places, a man who sees the Lord's qualities different from the Lord flows into the material world of** ***saṁsāra*****. Kaṭha Upaniṣad 2.1.14**  
**One who sees difference between the Lord and his qualities is criticized:**  
**The person who sees difference between the Lord and his qualities goes to the lowest ignorance. The person who sees difference and non-difference**  
**There is also the famous statement which seems to contradict qualities in the Lord:**  
**neha nānāsti kiñcana**  
**In the Lord, there is no difference. Bṛhad-ārāṇyaka Upaniṣad \|\|15\|\|**

### Text 16

*The qualities listed in the following are actually the Lord himself (dharmī):*  
*bhagavac-chabda-vācyāni vinā heyair guṇādibhiḥ \|\|16*

**Перевод:**

**The word** ***bhagavān*** **means that he is complete with power of mind (omniscience), power of senses, power of body, influence, control, and beauty, and is devoid of all inferior qualities.** ***Viṣṇu Purāṇa*** **6.5.79**

**The Lord is endowed with all auspicious qualities. He contains all beings by a particle of his śakti. He has an excellent body which he accepts by his will. He perfectly gives unlimited benefit to the world. (Viṣṇu Purāṇa 6.5.84)**

***guṇātmanas te ’pi guṇān vimātuṁ hitāvatīṛnasya ka īśire ’sya***  
***kālena yair vā vimitāḥ su-kalpair bhū-pāṁśavaḥ khe mihikā dyu-bhāsaḥ***

**In time, learned philosophers or scientists might be able to count all the atoms of the earth, the particles of snow, or perhaps even the shining molecules radiating from the sun, the stars and other luminaries. But among these learned men, who could possibly count the unlimited transcendental qualities possessed by you, the Supreme Lord, who have descended onto the surface of the earth for the benefit of all living entities? SB 10.14.7**

**Thus the possessor of the qualities (dharmī) is non-different from the qualities. There is difference in the non-difference like waves in the water. The Lord himself says:**  
**ubhaya-vyapadeśāt tv ahi-kuṇḍalavat**

**The Lord is both knowledge itself and qualified with knowledge, like a snake and its coils because scriptures say the Lord is both. Vedānta-sūtra 3.2.28 \|\|16\|\|**

### Text 17

*nanu sac-cid-ānanda-vigrahānatireki bhagavataḥ svarūpaṁ jñānānandādayo guṇāś ca tasmān nātiricyante iti svarūpato guṇataś ca nirbhinnaṁ tattvam iti nopayuktam \| saccidānanda-śabdānāṁ pravṛtti-nimitta-bhedena tad-vācyānāṁ saccidānandānāṁ bhedasyāvaśyakatvād iti cet, mandam etat \| acintye’laukike’rthe yukter anavatārāt \| paramaṁ tattvaṁ khalu tarka-gocaraṁ na bhavati, naiṣā tarkeṇety-ādi-śrutibhyaḥ, kintu śruty-eka-gamyam eva tat, yathāha bhagavān sūtra-kāraḥ—śrutes tu śabda-mūlatvāt \[bra.sū. 2.1.27\] iti \|\|17\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“You say that the svarūpa of the Lord who is non-different from saccidānanda is non-different from his qualities such as knowledge and bliss. But it is not proper to say that the Lord is nondifferent from his svarūpa and qualities. There must necessarily be difference between the qualities such as saccidānanda and the Lord, since the words saccidānanda originally refer to three concepts which are later applied to describe Brahman (pravṛṭti-nimitta-bhedena).”**

**This agrument is foolish since one cannot apply material logic to an object which is nonmaterial and inconceivable to the material senses and mind. The highest entity is not subject to material logic. The Lord is understood only by śruti.**

**naiṣā tarkeṇa matir āpaneyā proktānyenaiva sujñānāya preṣṭha**

**This knowledge cannot be achieved by logic. O dear one! It should be taught by another person in order to be understood properly. Kaṭha Upaniṣad 1.2.9**

**Vyāsa says:**  
**śrutes tu śabda-mūlatvāt**  
**The defects of the agent do not apply to Brahman because of scriptural statements, which are the only proof concerning inconceivable subjects. Vedānta-sūtra 2.1.27 \|\|17\|\|**

### Text 18

*svagatabhedo’pi tatra nety abhipretyāha śrutiḥ—neha nānāsti kiñcana iti \| smṛtiś ca— “nirdoṣa-pūrṇa-guṇa-vigraha ātma-tantro niścetanātmaka-śarīra-guṇaiś ca hīnaḥ \| ānanda-mātra-kara-pāda-mukhodarādiḥ sarvatra ca svagata-bheda-vivarjitātmā \|\|” \[nā.pa.\] iti \|*

*tathāpi vaidūryavad avicintyena viśeṣa-mahimnā taiḥ śabdair vyavahāro viduṣām api nirbodhaḥ \| na caivaṁ bhedābhedau syātāṁ niṣedhaka-vākya-vyākopāt \| tasmād avicintyataiva śaraṇam iti santoṣṭavyam \|\|18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Śruti denies internal difference in the Lord (*****svagata-bheda*****) such as his form and himself being one) as in** ***neha nānāsti kiñcana*****. The smṛtis confirm this:**  
**sarvatra ca svagata-bheda-vivarjitātmā \|\|**

**The Lord is a form with faultless, perfect qualities. He is independent. He is devoid of the qualities of the material body. His hands, feet, face and belly are all bliss. He is devoid of internal difference everywhere. (Nārada Pañcarātra)**

**This non-difference is arranged by the inconceivable power of the Lord, who is like a vaidūrya gem (which inconceivably manifests different colors at different times). Even intelligent persons cannot understand this. One should not consider this condition to arise from (the power of)** ***bhedābheda*** **itself, since the words of scripture deny this. One should take shelter of the** ***acintya*** **nature of the Lord. This is the satisfying conclusion. \|\|18\|\|**

### Text 19

*viśeṣas tv avaśyaṁ svīkāryaḥ \| sa ca bheda-pratinidhir bhedābhāve’pi bheda-kāryasya dharma-dharmi-bhāva-vyavahārasya satyādi-śabdāpara-paryāyatāyāś ca nirvartakaḥ, itarathā sattā satī bhedo bhinnaḥ kālaḥ sarvadā’sti deśaḥ sarvatrety abādhita-vyavahārānupapattiḥ \| pareṣām apy āvaśyakas tat-svīkāraḥ \| itarathā vijñānam ānandaṁ brahma \[bṛ.ā.u. 3.9.28\], satyaṁ jñānam anantaṁ brahma \[tai.u. 2.7\] ity-ādiṣu svarūpa-mātra-bodhakānāṁ vijñānādi-śabdānāṁ paryāyatāpattiḥ \| bhramādhiṣṭhānatvādinā caitanyasya sarvadā bhāne’pi tad-abhinnānandāder idānīm aprakāśānupapattiś ca \| svabhāvas tu viśeṣātmā \|\|19\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**But one must definitely accept distinctions (*****viśeṣa)*** **in the Lord (even though there is nondifference). A resemblance to difference, even where difference does not exist, is accomplished by making distinctions between the dharma and dharmī and by using other words such as** ***satya,*** **which are words with distinct meanings (they are not synonyms for Brahman but are actually descriptive elements or** ***viśeṣa*****). Otherwise, undisputed usages such as “Time exists at all times, the place is everywhere, the difference is distinct, and the existing condition exists” could not be accepted. Such usage is necessarily accepted by others.**

**And thus statements such as** ***vijñānam ānandaṁ brahma***  **and** ***satya jñānam anantaṁ brahma*** **cannot be used to indicate that** ***vijñāna*** **and other words are simply synonymous with the very nature of Brahman (“Brahman is knowledge and bliss” means “Brahman is Brahman,” and does not meant that Brahman has qualities of knowledge and bliss). According to this argument, though consciousness exists at all times, it becomes the substrate of illusion, and thus, ānanda, synonymous with Brahman, does not manifest at this time. But the very nature of Brahman is to have qualities. \|\|19\|\|**

### Text 20

*na ca vijñānatvādi-dharma-viśiṣṭābhidhāyibhir vijñānādi-śabdair viśiṣṭam abhidheyaṁ, śuddham akhaṇḍaṁ tu lakṣyam iti vācyam \| sarva-śabdānabhidheyasya tasya lakṣyatvāyogāt \| tathāpi tat tvam asīty atra śodhitāt padārthād vākyārthasyaikyasya bhedo bhedābhedo vā tava nābhimataḥ \| tathā saty aikyasya mithyātvādy-āpatteḥ \| tatra ced viśeṣo na syāt tarhi kathaṁ svaprakāśa-citprakāśe’py aikyasyāprakāśaḥ \| cit-prakāśasya bheda-bhramāvirodhitatve’pi tad-abhinnaikya-prakāśasya tad-virodhaś ceti tasmād avaśyābhyupeyo viśeṣaḥ \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One should not say that Brahman is pure and indivisible, and that words like** ***vijñāna*****, which are usually used to indicate that an object has the quality of** ***vijñāna*** **etc., are used only to indicate Brahman indirectly (*****lakṣya*****).**

**You do not accept difference (*****bheda*****) or difference with non-difference (*****bhedābheda*****) since there are statements of oneness in words like** ***tat tvam asi--*****provided that you “purify” the meaning of the two words** ***tat*** **and** ***tvam*** **to mean only Brahman. To accept difference or difference with nondifference would make the statements of oneness false. According to you, there is thus no qualities in Brahman.**

**But how can one explain that the oneness of Brahman becomes unmanifest (when illusion takes place), even though it is said to be self-manifesting consciousness. Though you may argue that the illusion of difference arising in Brahman is resolved on realizing oneness, Brahman is supposed to be a manifestation of pure consciousness but there is still a contradiction to Brahman as one undifferentiated entity (since it manifests difference in the form of an illusion of difference). Therefore viśeṣa (attribute, distinction) should be accepted necessarily. \|\|20\|\|**

### Text 21

*sa ca vastv abhinnaḥ sva-nirvāhakaś ceti nānavasthā \| tasya tathātvaṁ ca dharmi-grāhaka-pramāṇa-siddhām \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Since the attribute is non-different from the entity itself, which is self-accomplished, there is no state of anavasthā or a state of** ***ad infinitum*****,**

### Text 22

*nanu jīvatveśvaratvādi-vyavahāro’pi viśeṣād evāstu \| maivam \| yatra bhedābhāvo bheda-kāryaṁ ca pramite, tatraiva bheda-pratinidhi-viśeṣaḥ kalpyate, na tu pramita-bhedeṣu prakṛti-jīveṣu \| na hi soma-vallī-lābhe pūtikas tat-pratinidhiḥ kalpanīyaḥ, tasmād nirbhede’pi tattve bheda-vyavahāro viśeṣa-balād iti siddham \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“I propose that** ***viśeṣa*** **(distinction) causes a conventional perception of difference between the jīva and the Lord, (but that ultimately jīva and the Lord are one).” That cannot be. Where there is no distinction but a need to differentiate, one uses the device called** ***viśeṣa.*** **But this is not a subtitute for the actual differences between the Lord and prakṛṭi and jīvas, which are proven in the scripture One cannot substitute a pūtika plant for a vine of the soma plant. Thus it is established that common perception of distinction within Brahman which has no differences within it arises from** ***viśeṣa*****. \|\|22\|\|**

### Text 23

*atrāhuḥ vigraho guṇāś ca svarūpād atiricyante \| itarathā yad-ātmako bhagavaṁs tad-ātmikā vyaktiḥ, ānandaṁ brahmaṇo vidvān \[tai.u.2.6.1\] ity-ādi-śruteḥ \| mama dehe guḍākeśa \[gītā. 11.7\], harer guṇākṣipta-matir \[bhā.pu. 1.7.11\] ity-ādi-smṛteś ca svārasya-bhaṅgaḥ \| na ca vigrahāder api jñānādi-rūpatvaṁ niṣprayojanaṁ pramāṇena prayojana-mukhānirīkṣaṇāt \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Some say the form and qualities are different from the svarūpa of Brahman. Otherwise scriptural statements would not make sense. There are śruti statements such as the following (which distinguish the Lord’s svarūpa from his form and qualities):**  
**ānandaṁ brahmaṇo vidvānO**

**ne knows the bliss of Brahman. (Taittirīya Upaniṣad)**  
***yad ātmako bhagavāṁs tad-ātmikā vyaktiḥ kim ātmako bhagavān jñānātmaka aiśvaryātmakaḥ śaktyātmakaḥ*****:**  
**What constitutes the Lord becomes manifest as non-different from himself. What constitutes the Lord? The Lord’s very essence is knowledge, power and specific śaktis. (quoted by Madhva)**

**There are smṛti statements: mama dehe guḍākeśa O Arjuna, see in my body the universe.**

**harer guṇakṣipta-matiḥ**  
**Śukadeva’s mind became distracted from trance by the qualities of Kṛṣṇa. SB 1.7.11**  
**But one should not argue that knowledge taking a form is without a purpose. Scriptural proof does not depend on something have a specific purpose. \|\|23\|\|**

### Text 24

*viśeṣeṇa svarūpeṇa saha tasya sambandho nityo’pṛthak-siddha-viśeṣaṇatvāt \| nitya-sambandhāpekṣīṇy eva svarūpānatirekitva-vacāṁsi \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The relationship of the Lord’s svarūpa with his form and qualities is eternal since the viśeṣas are inherently related to the svarūpa. There are scriptural statements stating that the qualities are intrinsic to Lord’s svarūpa and have an eternal relationship with him. \|\|24\|\|**

### Text 25

*na ca viśeṣya-viśeṣaṇayor jñānādi-rūpatve nitya-sambandhe ca svīkṛte bhedānupapattir viśeṣyatva-viśeṣaṇatvābhyāṁ tayor bhedāt \| ekaṁ jñānādimat parantu jñānādi-rūpam iti \| itarathā jīveśayor abhedāpattiḥ \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Though accepting the eternal relationship of the Lord and his qualities in the form of knowledge etc.** ***bheda*** **is still established, because one must accept difference between the Lord and his attributes. The Lord is endowed with knowledge (quality), though he is also the very form of knowledge. If the difference between the Lord and his qualities is not accepted, the Lord and the jīva (the Lord’s attribute) would also have to be accepted as non-different. \|\|25\|\|**

### Text 26

*yathodakaṁ durge vṛṣṭam \[ka.u.4.14.\] ity ādyā śrutis tu pṛthak-siddhi-vādinaṁ nindati \| tasmāt svarūpād vigrahasya guṇānāṁ ca bheda eveti \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The śrutis criticize the proposition that the Lord has material qualities.**

**yathodakaṁ durge vṛṣṭaṁ parvateṣu vidhāvati**  
**evāṁ dharmān pṛthak paśyaṁs tān evānuvidhāvati**

**As water falling on mountains flows to inaccessible places, a man who sees the Lord's qualities different from the Lord flows into the material world of** ***saṁsāra*****. Kaṭha Upaniṣad 2.1.14**  
**Therefore, there is difference between the qualities of the Lord’s form and his svarūpa. \|\|26\|\|**

### Text 27

*naitad aticāru yad ity ādi śruti-smṛti-svārasya-bhaṅgaāt \| śrutes tu śabda-mūlatvāt \[bra.sū. 2.1.27\] iti hi naḥ sthitir iti \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Claims of complete nondifference are not attractive, since they destroy the natural meaning of the śruti and smṛti. Our position is based on scriptures:**  
**śrutes tu śabda-mūlatvāt**

**The defects of the agent do not apply to Brahman because of scriptural statements, which are the only proof concerning inconceivable subjects. Vedānta-sūtra 2.1.27 \|\|27\|\|**

### Text 28

*sa-guṇa-vādi-vākyānāṁ guṇānuvāditvena guṇa-vidhāne tātparyābhāvān nirguṇaṁ caitanyam eva pāramārthikaṁ, na tu saguṇaṁ brahmeti duruktis tūpariṣṭān nirākariṣyate \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By citing statements about Brahman with qualities (*****saguna-brahman*****), while explaining the meaning of the statements about qualities, some persons wrongfully proclaim that consciousness without qualities is spiritual and Brahman with qualities (*****saguṇa-brahman*****) is not. This will be refuted later (chapter four). \|\|28\|\|**

### Text 29

*tathā ca vigrahādeḥ svarūpānatireke’pi viśeṣād eva bheda-vyavahāraḥ \| pravṛtti-nimitta-bhedād artha-bhedas tu laukiko’smābhiḥ svīkṛtaḥ \| na tv alaukike śruti-mātra-gocare parasmiṁs tattve sa svīkartuṁ śakyate \|\|29\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Though the svarūpa of the Lord is non-different from his form, difference (between the Lord and his quality) is perceived because of** ***viśeṣa*****. We accept difference in the meaning of a word according to the object it describes (*****pravṛtti-nimitta*****) in this world, but we cannot accept such rules in relation to the supreme spiritual entity, accessible only by śruti. \|\|29\|\|**

### Text 30

*tad evaṁ tathābhūtasya bhagavato jñānād evāsya jīvasya tayor ātyantikayoḥ siddhir na tv anyasmād iti siddhyati, tam eva viditvā atimṛtyum eti nānyaḥ panthā vidyate’yanāya \[śve. u. 3.8\] iti śravaṇāt, vidyaiva tu tan-nirdhāraṇāt \[bra.sū. 3.3.47\] iti nyāyāc ca \| plavā hy ete adṛḍhā yajña-rūpāḥ \[mu.u. 1.2.7\], nāsty akṛtaḥ kṛtena \[mu.u. 1.2.12\], na karmaṇā na prajayā dhanena tyāgenaike’mṛtatvam ānaśuḥ \[ma.nā.u. 10\], “yathādṛḍhaiḥ karma-mayaiḥ kratubhir nāma-nau-nibhaiḥ \| vidyām ānvīkṣikīṁ hitvā titīrṣanti bhavārṇavam \|\|”s \[bhā.10.25.4\] ity-ādi-śruti-smṛtibhyaḥ \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, by knowledge of the Lord who has qualities intrinsic to himself, the jīva attains complete destruction of suffering and eternal bliss. It is not otherwise. This is stated in śrutis and smṛtis.**

**tam eva viditvā atimṛtyum eti nānyaḥ panthā vidyate’yanāya**

**vidyaiva tu tan-nirdhāraṇāt**  
**plavā hy ete adṛḍhā yajña-rūpāḥ**

**Those who practice inferior** ***karma*** **are like unsteady boats with their absorption in sacrifices. Muṇḍaka Upaniṣad 1.2.7**  
**nāsty akṛtaḥ kṛtena**  
**One cannot attain freedom from karma by acts of karma. Muṇḍaka Upaniṣad 1.2.12**

**na karmaṇā na prajayā dhanena tyāgenaike’mṛtatvam ānaśuḥ**  
**Not by karmas, sons or wealth, only by renunciation, can one attain immortality. Taittarīya-āraṇyaka 10.5**

**vidyām ānvīkṣikīṁ hitvā titīrṣanti bhavārṇavam \|\|**  
**Their taking shelter of Kṛṣṇa is just like the foolish attempt of men who abandon transcendental knowledge of the self and instead try to cross over the great ocean of material existence in the false boats of fruitive, ritual sacrifices. SB 10.25.4 \|\|30\|\|**

### Text 31

*syād etat \| “yogās trayo mayā proktā nṝṇāṁ śreyo-vidhitsayā \| jñānaṁ karma ca bhaktiś ca nopāyo’nyo’sti kutracit \|\|” \[bhā.11.20.6\] iti bhagavatā trikaṁ niḥśreyasa-hetur ity uktam \| vidyaiva tad-dhetur iti katham ? iti cet, ucyate—karma ṇaḥ sākṣād-dhetutvaṁ nāsti, pūrvokta-śruty-ādibhyaḥ, kintu citta-śuddhi-dvārā tad ubhayāṅgataiva, “tam etaṁ vedānuvacanena brahmaṇā vividiṣanti yajñena dānena tapasānāśakena” \[bṛ.ā.u. 4.4.22\] ity-ādi-śruteḥ \| “yathecchāvāsa-niratā sarva-bādhā-vivarjitāḥ \| śuddhāntaḥ-karaṇāḥ śuddhāḥ karmānuṣṭhāna-nirmalāḥ \|\| śuddhe ca tāsāṁ manasi śuddhe’ntaḥ-saṁsthite harau \| śuddha-jñānaṁ prapaśyanti viṣṇv-ākhyaṁ yena tat-padam \|\|” \[vi.pu. 1.6.12-13\] ity-ādi-smṛteś ca \| kintu jñāna-bhaktyor eva sākṣād-dhetutvam \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord speaks of three paths:**  
**jñānaṁ karma ca bhaktiś ca nopāyo’nyo’sti kutracit \|\|**

**Because I desire that human beings may achieve perfection, I have presented three methods—the path of** ***jñāna,*** **the path of** ***karma*** **and the path of** ***bhakti*****. Besides these three, there are no other means of elevation. SB 11.20.6**

**Then why is it said that vidyā is the cause of liberation? Karma is not a direct path for us according to the śrutis just cited. It is a means of purifying citta. It thus becomes an aṅga of jñāna and bhakti.**  
**tam etaṁ vedānuvacanena brahmaṇā vividiṣanti yajñena dānena tapasānāśakena**

**The seekers of Brahman try to know him by study of the Vedas (*****brahmacārī),*** **by sacrifices, by acts of charity (*****grhastha*****), by austerities (*****vanaprastha*****) and by fasting (*****sannyāsī*****). (Bṛhad-āraṇyaka Upaniṣad 4.4)**

**Joyful in living here and there, devoid of all disturbances, having a pure antaḥkaraṇa, pure in body, purified by performance of karmas, when the pure Lord becomes situated in their pure minds, persons realize pure knowledge, called Viṣṇu, by which they attain his place. Viṣṇu Purāṇa**  
**Bhakti and jñāna are direct causes of liberation. \|\|31\|\|**

### Text 32

*bhaktir api jñāna-viśeṣo bhavatīti jñānatva-sāmānyāt tam eveti vidyaiveti vyapadeśaḥ \| jātiṁ puraskṛtya bahuṣv aikyaṁ vyapadiśyate \| yad āha bharataḥ—rasādīnām anantatvād bheda eko hi gaṇyate iti \| vivṛtaṁ caitat \| asaṁlakṣya-kramatvaṁ jātim ādāya rasādir eka eva gaṇyate \| anyathā tad-gaṇanam aśakyaṁ pratyekaṁ bheda-bāhulyād iti \| jñāna-viśeṣe bhakti-śabdasya prayogaḥ kaurava-viśeṣe pāṇḍava-śabdavad bodhyaḥ \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Bhakti is also a special type of knowledge or jñāna. Jñāna indicates knowing in general in statements like** ***tam eva viditvā*** **(knowing the Lord) and** ***vidyaiva tu tan-nirdhāraṇāt*** **quoted above. One word can indicate many types. Bhārata says** ***rasādīnām anantatvād bheda eko hi gaṇyate*****: though there are many types of rasa, only one type is considered. Taking the type without considering the whole series, one rasa is considered. Otherwise it would be impossible to enumerate each one, since there are so many. The use of the word** ***bhakti*** **as a special type of jñāna is like using the word Pāṇḍava as a special type of Kaurava. \|\|32\|\|**

### Text 33

*ayam atra niṣkarṣaḥ \| vidyā-vedana-paryāyaṁ jñānaṁ dvividhaṁ \| ekaṁ nirṇimeṣa-vīkṣaṇavāt tattvaṁ padārthānubhava-rūpaṁ, dvitīyaṁ tv apāṅga-vīkṣaṇavad vicitraṁ bhakti-rūpam iti \| tatra śuddhatvaṁ padārthānusandhinā’sya jīvasya tat-pada-bhājo’pi pati-tyakta-patnīvad bhagavat-prasāda-saubhāgya-hīnasya kaivalya-lakṣaṇo mokṣaḥ syāt \| “ātmānaṁ ced vijānīyād ayam asmīti pūruṣaḥ \| kim icchan kasya kāmāya śarīram anusaṁjvaret \|\|” \[bṛ.ā.u. 4.4.12\] ity-ādi-śruteḥ \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This is the meaning. There are two types of jñāna for attaining vidyā. One type of jñāna is realizing the truth, general nature of existence. This is like looking with unblinking eyes. The other type of jñāna or knowledge is bhakti. This is like looking with a side glance.**

**By inquiry into essential existence (jñāna) one attains purity. The jīva, attaining that position, attains liberation, characterized by oneness, devoid of the good fortune of the Lord’s mercy. This condition is like a wife who has been abandoned by her husband.**  
**kim icchan kasya kāmāya śarīram anusaṁjvaret \|\|**  
**If a person knows the true nature of the ātmā, and if he thinks, \`I am spirit', then what will he desire in this material world? How will he become attached to his material body? Bṛhad-ārāṇyaka Upaniṣad 4.4.12 \|\|33\|\|**

### Text 34

*tat-padārtha-pariśuddha-vijñānāttv amātya-sainyādhipādivad yathāyathaṁ tat-prasāda-saubhāgya-bhājanasya tasya sālokyādi-lakṣaṇā muktir bhavati \| etad vai satyakāma paraṁ cāparaṁ brahma yad oṅkāra ity upakramya, yaḥ punar etaṁ trimātreṇaum ity anenaivākṣareṇa paraṁ puruṣam abhidhyā īta sa tejasi sūrye sampanno yathā pādodaras tvacā vinirmucyate evaṁ haiva sa pāpmabhir vinirmuktaḥ sa sāmabhir unnīyate brahma-lokaṁ sa etasmāj jīva-ghanāt parātparaṁ puriśayaṁ puruṣam īkṣate iti śravaṇāt \| śāstrīya-jñānaṁ tv atra paratra ca dvāra-bhūtaṁ svīkāryam itarathā pravṛtty-anupapatteḥ \|\|34\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**From pure realization of the Lord, the practitioner, like minister or general, becomes the object of the Lord’s mercy and attains types of liberation such as sālokya.**  
**yaḥ punar etaṁ trimātreṇaum ity anenaivākṣareṇa paraṁ puruṣam abhidhyā īta sa tejasi sūrye sampanno yathā pādodaras tvacā vinirmucyate evaṁ haiva sa pāpmabhir vinirmuktaḥ sa sāmabhir unnīyate brahma-lokaṁ sa etasmāj jīva-ghanāt parātparaṁ puriśayaṁ puruṣam īkṣate**

**He who by the three syllables of oṁ meditates on the supreme person attains the bright sun. As a snake becomes freed from its old skin, this person becomes freed of sin. He rises to Brahmaloka by chanting the Sāma verses. He sees that person in his abode, that person who is superior to the mass of jīvas. Praśna Upaniṣad 5.5**

**One must accept scriptural knowledge concerning the nature of the Lord and bhakti as the door to that liberation. Without that knowledge, one cannot practice. \|\|34\|\|**

### Text 35

*bhakti-rūpeṇa tu jñāna-viśeṣeṇa sneha-saundaryādi-guṇāñcita-yuvati-ratna-vad bhagavad-vaśīkāra-prasāda-pātrasya tad-aṅghri-varivasyānanda-lakṣaṇaḥ puruṣārtho bhavati \| bhaktir evainaṁ nayati bhaktir evainaṁ darśayati bhakti-vaśaḥ puruṣo bhaktir eva bhūyasī \[māṭhara śruti\] iti, vijñāna-ghanānanda-ghanā saccidānandaika-rase bhakti-yoge tiṣṭhati \[go.tā.u.\] iti ca śruteḥ, “ahaṁ bhakta-parādhīno hy asvatantra iva dvija \| sādhubhir grasta-hṛdayo bhaktair bhakta-jana-priyaḥ \|\|” “mayi nibaddha-hṛdayāḥ sādhavaḥ sama-darśinaḥ \| vaśe kurvanti māṁ bhāktyā sat-striyaḥ sat-patiṁ yathā \|\|” \[bhā.pu. 9.4.63, 66\] ity ādi-smṛteś ca \|\|35\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The person who, by special jñāṇa in the form of bhakti, becomes the object of mercy which controls the Lord--who is a youthful gem endowed with qualities like affection and beauty-- attains the highest goal, characterized by bliss in serving the Lord’s lotus feet.**

**bhaktir evainaṁ nayati bhaktir evainaṁ darśayati bhakti-vaśaḥ puruṣo bhaktir eva bhūyasī**

**Bhakti alone attracts the Lord. Bhakti alone reveals the Lord. The Lord is controlled by bhakti. Bhakti alone is great. (Māṭhara-śruti)**

**vijñāna-ghanānanda-ghana saccidānandaika-rase bhakti-yoge tiṣṭhati**

**The form of Kṛṣṇa, filled with knowledge and bliss, resides in bhakti-yoga, which is also full of eternity, knowledge and bliss. Gopāla-tāpanī Upaniṣad**

**O** ***brāhmaṇa*****! I am completely under the control of my devotees. I am not at all independent. My heart is controlled by the pure devotees. What to speak of my devotee, even those who are devotees of my devotee are very dear to me. SB 9.4.63**  
**vaśe kurvanti māṁ bhāktyā sat-striyaḥ sat-patiṁ yathā \|\|**

**As chaste women bring their gentle husbands under control by service, the pure devotees, who see others’ suffering as their own and are completely attached to me in the core of the heart, bring me under their full control. SB 9.4.66 \|\|35\|\|**

### Text 36

*sālokyādes tu nehābhilāṣaḥ svataḥ sambhavāt \| “matsevayā pratītaṁ te sālokyādi-catuṣṭayam \|*

**Перевод:**

**The devotee does not desire sālokya but it is attained on its own.**  
**necchanti sevayā pūrṇāḥ kuto’nyat kāla-viplutam \|\|**

**My devotees, who are always satisfied to be engaged in my service, are not interested even in the four principles of liberation \[sālokya, sārūpya, sāmīpya and sārṣṭi\], although these are automatically achieved by their service. What then is to be said of any perishable happiness? SB 9.4.67**  
**This lack of material desire is described among the characteristics of bhakti.**

**bhaktir asya bhajanam \| tad ihāmutropādhi-nairasyenāmuṣmin manaḥ-kalpanam etad eva naiṣkarmyam**

**Bhajanam or service means devotional service to Kṛṣṇa. It means concentration of the mind on Kṛṣṇa without desires for enjoyment in this life or the next. It destroys all karma. Gopāla-tāpanī Upaniṣad**  
**hṛṣīkeṇa hṛṣīkeśa-sevanaṁ bhaktir ucyate \|\|**

### Text 37

*“evaṁ ca yathā-kratur asmiṁl loke puruṣo bhavati, tathetaḥ pretya bhavati,” “ye yathā māṁ prapadyante tāṁs tathaiva bhajāmy aham” \[gītā 4.11\] “nāyaṁ sukhāpo bhagavān dehināṁ gopikā-sutaḥ \| jñānināṁ cātma-bhūtānāṁ yathā bhaktimatām iha \|\| “\[bhā.10.9.21\] ity-ādīni phala-tāratamya-vākyāni saṅgaccherann iti \|\|37\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Here are some verses indicated the various results.**

**yathā-kratur asmiṁ loke puruṣo bhavati, tathetaḥ pretya bhavati.**

**As a person develops according to his faith in this world, so he attains in the next life. Chāndogya Upaniṣad 3.14.1**  
**ye yathā māṁ prapadyante tāṁs tathaiva bhajāmy aham**  
**As one surrenders to me, I respond accordingly. BG 4.11**

**jñānināṁ cātma-bhūtānāṁ yathā bhaktimatām iha \|\|**

**The Supreme Lord, the son of mother Yaśodā, is accessible to devotees engaged in spontaneous loving service, but he is not as easily accessible to mental speculators, to those striving for self-realization by severe austerities and penances, or to those who consider the body the same as the self. SB 10.9.21**

### Text 38

*atraivaṁ punaś cintyate bhagavad-vaśīkāra-hetu-bhūtā śaktiḥ kiṁ svarūpeti kiṁ prākṛta-sattvamaya-jñānānanda-rūpā ? kiṁ vā bhagavat-svarūpa-jñānānanda-rūpā ? athavā jaiva-jñānānanda-rūpā ? uta hlādinī-sāra-samaveta-saṁvit-sārarūpā ? iti \| nādyaḥ, bhagavato māyā-vaśyatvāśravaṇāt, svataḥ pūrṇatvāc ca \| na dvitīyo’tiśayāsiddheḥ \| nāpi tṛtīyo jaivayos tayoḥ kṣodiṣṭhatvāt \| kintu caturtha evāsau bhavet \|\|38\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In this, one should consider “What is the svarūpa of bhakti, which is a śakti which controls the Lord? Is bhakti a form of knowledge and bliss arising from material sattva? Or is bhakti the knowledge and bliss which are the very svarūpa of the Lord? Or is it the knowledge and bliss of the jīva? Or is bhakti the essence of hlādinī and saṁvit?”**

**Bhakti does not arise from material sattva, since the Lord is not controlled by māyā but rather he is complete in himself. Bhakti is not knowledge and bliss of the Lord’s svarūpa, since how could the Lord (in his svarūpa) be greater than the Lord? Bhakti is not the jīva’s knowledge and bliss, since the jīva’s knowledge and bliss are very meager. Bhakti must be the essence of the hlādinī and saṁvit śaktis. \|\|38\|\|**

### Text 39

*bhaktau khalu bhagavān svayam eva vaśībhūtas tiṣṭhati tāmarasa-kośe madhupa iva rasika-yuvatyāṁ rasika-yuveveti śruti-smṛtibhyaḥ pratīyate \| tat-pratītārthānyathānupapatti-prasūtayārthāpattyā tu “hlādinī sandhinī saṁvit tvayy ekā sarva-saṁśraye \| hlāda-tāpa-karī miśrā tvayi no guṇa-varjite \|\|” \[vi.pu. 1.12.69\] iti śri-viṣṇu-purāṇokta-svarūpa-viśeṣa-bhūta-hlādinī-saṁvit-sāra-rūpā seti niścīyate \|\|39\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord is indeed controlled by bhakti. He is like a bee absorbed in a lotus bud or like an amorous youthful man enjoying with an amorous youthful woman. This is recognized by the śrutis and smṛtis. But the fact that bhakti is the essence of his śaktis is understood by artha-patti,**

**hlādinī sandhinī saṁvit tvayy ekā sarva-saṁśraye \|**  
**hlāda-tāpa-karī miśrā tvayi no guṇa-varjite \|\|**

**In you who are the shelter of all spiritual qualities exist the energies of hlādinī, sandhinī and saṁvit. In you who are devoid of material qualities, the mixture of happiness and distress found in the material world does not exist. (Viṣṇu Purāṇa 1.9.45) \|\|39\|\|**

### Text 40

*tathā ca hlāda-saṁvidoḥ sāro bhaktir iti sidhyati \| tat-sāratvaṁ ca tan-nitya-parikarāśrayaka-tad-ānukūlyābhilāṣa-viśeṣaḥ \| ithambhūtā bhaktir ity abhiprāyeṇaiva saccidānandaika-rasatayā tāṁ viśinaṣṭi śrutis tasyā bhagavad-vaśyatāṁ ca darśayati—vijñāna-ghanā ity ādinā, bhaktir evainaṁ nayati ity ādinā \|\|40\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**It has been established that bhakti is the essence of hlādinī and saṁvit śaktis. As the essence (sāra), bhakti is a special desire, favorable to the Lord, which takes shelter of his eternal associates. In this manner, the śrutis describe bhakti as being the rasa of saccidānanda, and they show that bhakti controls the Lord in such verses as the following:**  
**bhaktir evainaṁ nayati bhaktir evainaṁ darśayati bhakti-vaśaḥ puruṣo bhaktir eva bhūyasī**  
**Bhakti alone attracts the Lord. Bhakti alone reveals the Lord. The Lord is controlled by bhakti. Bhakti alone is great. (Māṭhara-śruti)**

**vijñāna-ghanānanda-ghana saccidānandaika-rase bhakti-yoge tiṣṭhati**

**The form of Kṛṣṇa, filled with knowledge and bliss, resides in bhakti-yoga, which is also full of eternity, knowledge and bliss. Gopāla-tāpanī Upaniṣad \|\|40\|\|**

### Text 41

*nanu hlādiny-ādi-rūpā sā bhaktir bhagavat-svarūpa-bhūtāj jñānānandān nātiricyate, yad asau svarūpa-śaktir iti bhavadbhir evoktam \| katham adhunā bhagavat-svarūpa-jñānānandatvaṁ bhakteḥ pratyākhyāyata iti cet \| atrācakṣmahe na khalu parābhyupagata-nirviśeṣa-caitanya-mātravad brahma-svarūpaṁ svīkurmahe kintu svarūpa-śaktimad eva, śaktiś ca svarūpānatirekiṇy api tad-viśeṣatayāvabhāsate, anyathā tasya śaktir iti vyapadeśāsiddheḥ \|\|41\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Bhakti composed of hlādinī and saṁvit is none other than knowledge and bliss since it arises from the svarūpa of the Lord. You have said that bhakti is the Lord’s svarūpa-śakti. Why is bhakti not simply accepted as the Lord’s knowledge and bliss?”**

**This is explained as follows. We do not accept that the Lord is merely Brahman, undifferentiated consciousness, which is what others accept. Rather we accept that the Lord has a svarūpa endowed with śakti. The śakti is non-different from the Lord’s svarūpa, but yet manifests as the Lord’s particulars (*****viśeṣa*****). If this were not so, it would not be called “his” śakti. \|\|41\|\|**

### Text 42

*avayavāvayavi-bhāvavad eṣa pratyayaḥ—yathā kara-caraṇādy-avayavinaḥ karādy-avayavānām anatireke’pi tad-abhāva-pratītir vaiduṣī, tathaitad draṣṭavyam \| na hy eṣā nirmūlā kalpanā neha nānāsti kiñcana ity-ādi-śruti-smṛtibhyaḥ \| “pratiṣedhāc ca” \[bra.sū.\] iti nyāyāc ca \| tathā ca vartulatva-komalatvādi-viśiṣṭo yūno bhujo yathā yuvatyāḥ skandhe nihitas tayoḥ paramānandāya samprāpte tathā bhagavat-svarūpa-viśeṣa-bhūta-hlādiny-ādi-sārātmā bhaktir bhagavaty apṛthag-viśeṣaṇatayā, bhakte ca pṛthag-viśeṣaṇatayā siddhā tayor ānandātiśayāya bhavatīti sarvaṁ niravadyam \|\|42\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The relation of the śakti to the Lord (Brahman) is like the relationship of a limb to a body. There is non-difference between the limbs such as hands and the body possessing the hands and other limbs. The absence of difference is the perception of the wise. That should be seen. This is not a baseless fancy, since śrutis and smṛtis proclaim this with statements like** ***neha nānāstikiñcana*****. Vedānta-sūtra says: pratiṣedhāc ca**

**The Lord and his qualities are non-different because difference is rejected by scripture. Vedānta-sūtra 3.2.31**

**When a young man’s soft, well-formed hand is placed on a young girls shoulder, they both experience intense bliss. Similarly, bhakti the essence of the hlādinī and saṁvit śaktis, arising from the svarūpa of the Lord, being non-different from the Lord, and being particularized as different in the devotee, gives great bliss to the Lord and the devotee. Thus there is no fault. \|\|42\|\|**

### Text 43

*atra sandhinī-saṁvid-dhlādinyo yathottaram utkṛṣṭā jñeyāḥ \| tatra sad-ātmāpi yayā sattāṁ dhatte dadāti ca, sā sarva-deśa-kāla-dravya-vyāpti-hetuḥ sandhinī \| saṁvid-ātmāpi yayā saṁvetti saṁvedayati ca sā saṁvit \| hlādātmāpi yayā hlādate hlādayati ca, sā hlādinīti tāt-prādhānyena sphūrtes tat-tad-rūpatā tasyā ekasyā vaidūryavad avasīyate \|\|43\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Sandhinī, saṁvit and hlādinī are progressively more excellent. Sandhinī is composed of existence. By it, things exist. It is the cause of existence of all places, times and objects. Saṁvit-śakti causes knowledge. By hlādinī one experiences bliss and one causes bliss. One śakti takes these form by manifesting predominance of one of these natures. \|\|43\|\|**

### Text 44

*sā tad-ubhaya-sārāṁśa-rūpā rati-premākhyā bhaktir bhakte bhagavati cātmānaṁ nikṣipya mithas tāv anurañjayati \| yad uktam—“sādhavo hṛdayaṁ mahyaṁ sādhūnāṁ hṛdayaṁ tv aham \| mad-anyat te na jānanti nāhaṁ tebhyo manāg api \|\|” \[bhā.pu. 9.4.68\] iti, “visṛjati hṛdayaṁ na yasya sākṣād dharir avaśābhihito’py aghaugha-nāśaḥ \| praṇaya-rasanayā dhṛtāṅghri-padmaḥ sa bhavati bhāgavata-pradhāna uktaḥ \|\|” \[bhā.pu. 11.2.55\] iti ca, bhakteṣu bhagavad-bhāktiś ca darśitā—“evaṁ sva-bhaktayo rājan bhagavān bhakta-bhaktimān \| uṣitvādiśya san-mārgaṁ punar dvāravatīm agāt \|\|” \[bhā.pu. 10.86.59\] ity ādau \|\|44\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Bhakti, called rati and prema, the essence of the saṁvit and hlādinī śaktis, delivers itself to the devotee and the Lord. The two become attracted to each other.**

**The pure devotee is my very heart: what pains him pains me. I am the heart of the pure devotee: what pleases him pleases me. My devotees do not know anything else but me, and I do not know more than they do. SB 9.4.68**

**He who binds the lotus feet of the Lord by ropes of love, and whose heart the Lord, destroyer of heaps of sin, does not leave, even if beckoned accidentally, is called the best of devotees. SB 11.2.55**

**The devotion of the Lord for his devotees is shown:**

**uṣitvādiśya san-mārgaṁ punar dvāravatīm agāt \|\|**

**O King, thus the Lord, who is devoted to his own devotees, stayed for some time with his two great devotees Śrutadeva and Bahulāśva, teaching them the behavior of perfect saints. Then the Lord returned to Dvārakā. SB 10.86.59 \|\|44\|\|**

### Text 45

*ata eva bhaktānandāya tasya vividhaḥ prayatno’pi “muhūrtenāpi saṁhartuṁ śakto yadyapi dānavān \| mad-bhāktānāṁ vinodārthaṁ karomi vividhāḥ kriyāḥ \|\| darśana-dhyāna-saṁsparśaiḥ matsya-kūrma-vihaṅgamāḥ \| svāny apatyāni puṣṇanti tathāham api padmaja \|\|” \[padma-purāṇa\] iti tad-ukteḥ \|\|45\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, the Lord makes endeavors for the happiness of the devotee:**

**muhūrtenāpi saṁhartuṁ śakto yadyapi dānavān \|**  
**mad-bhāktānāṁ vinodārthaṁ karomi vividhāḥ kriyāḥ \|\|**  
**darśana-dhyāna-saṁsparśaiḥ matsya-kūrma-vihaṅgamāḥ \|**  
**svāny apatyāni puṣṇanti tathāham api padmaja**

### Text 46

*tasmāt paramānanda-mayo’pi yayānandātiśayī bhavati svarūpānandaṁ cānubhavati taṁ tam anubhāvayati ca bhaktān \| tasyāḥ sattva-mayānandādi-rūpatvaṁ katham iti parama-pumartha-rūpatvaṁ bhakteḥ siddhām \| jñāna-phalaṁ bhagavān bhakti-phalaṁ tu bhaktir eva tad-viṣayā tasyā evodagrānandatvāt \|\|46\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Thought the Lord is filled with the highest bliss, by which, being blissful, he experiences the bliss of his svarūpa, he makes his devotees experience that bliss. How can bhakti be the bliss of material sattva? It has been established that bhakti is the highest human goal. The result of knowledge is the Lord but the result of bhakti is bhakti (prema) aimed at the Lord, since it is the cause of the highest bliss. \|\|46\|\|**

### Text 47

*ānandādi-rūpāyās tasyāḥ kāyādi-tādātmyenāvirbhūtāyāḥ kriyā-kāratvam ānanda-cin-mūrteḥ kuntalādi-pratīkatvavad avaseyam \| kupita-pittena sitā-mādhuryam ivāvicchinna-saṁsāreṇa tadādi-rūpatvaṁ tasyāḥ pūrvaṁ nānubhūyate tat-saṁsevayā tu krameṇa tathātvam anubhūyata iti \|\|47\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Bhakti is a form of bliss and manifests by becoming one with the body and senses. The activities of the hand or other limbs (imbued with bhakti) are understood to be like the Lord’s locks of hair and his limbs which reveal the form of the Lord who is filled with knowledge and bliss.**

**A person with disturbed pitta cannot taste the sweetness of sugar. Similarly a jīva, because of being in saṁsāra continuously, cannot experience the form and other aspects of the Lord. But by service to the Lord, gradually he can experience the true nature of the Lord. \|\|47\|\|**

### Text 48

*ata eva siddhānāṁ tasyā pravṛtty-anuparama ity āha bhagavān sūtra-kāraḥ—āprāyaṇāt tatrāpi hi dṛṣṭam \[bra.sū. 4.1.12\] iti \| na caiva bhakti-puruṣārtha-vādināṁ bhagavat-puruṣārthatā-vigamaḥ tasyās tad-avinābhāvena tathaiva tasyāpi prāpteḥ \| tathā ca bhaktimatāṁ bhakti-viṣayo bhagavān puruṣārtha iti niṣkṛṣṭam iti \|\|48\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The perfected souls do not give up bhakti. The author of the sūtras, the Lord,** ***say:***  
***āprāyaṇāt tatrāpi hi dṛṣṭam***

**One should worship the Lord up till liberation and also after liberation because that is stated in scriptures. (Vedānta-sūtra 4.1.12)**

**Those who accept bhakti as the goal do not give up accepting the Lord as the goal, since they invariably attain the Lord by bhakti. They are interconnected. For the devotees, the object of devotion is the Lord. \|\|48\|\|**

### Text 49

*tasmāt karmaṇaś citta-śuddhāv upayogaḥ jñāna-bhakti-rūpāyā vidyāyās tu sākṣāt tat-prāptāv iti na kācid vikṣatiḥ \|\|49\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One engages in karma-yoga for purification of citta. By knowledge through jñāna or bhakti, one attains the Lord directly. There is no harm in such a statement. \|\|49\|\|**

### Text 50

*ye tu kecit sad-viśeṣa-prasaṅgād ārambhata eva bhakti-prāptās te punar bhaktyaiva citta-śuddhiṁ tat-sākṣāt-kṛtiṁ ca bhajante, “pibanti ye bhagavata ātmanaḥ satāṁ kathāmṛtaṁ śravaṇa-puṭeṣu sambhṛtam \| punanti te viṣaya-vidūṣitāśayaṁ vrajanti tac-caraṇa-saroruhāntikam \|\|” \[bhā.pu. 2.2.37\] ity-ādi-śravaṇāt \| etān uddiśya śrutir āha— “tapasā brahmacaryeṇa śuddhayā vidyayātmānam anviśet” \[pra.u. 1.10\] iti \|\|50\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**But those who start bhakti through the association with devotees attain purification by bhakti alone (not from karma-yoga), and attain the Lord directly.**

**vrajanti tac-caraṇa-saroruhāntikam \|\|**

**The same is stated in the śrutis:**

**tapasā brahmacaryeṇa śuddhayā vidyayātmānam anviśet**

**By austerity, brahmacarya, purity and knowledge one should desire the Lord. Praśna Upaniṣad 1.10 \|\|50\|\|**

### Text 51

*eṣā khalu bhaktir ahaituky eva tāṁ sādhayati na tv anyādṛśī \| “ahaituky avyavahitā yā bhaktiḥ puruṣottame \|\| sālokya-sārṣṭi-sāmīpya-sārūpyaikatvam apy uta \| dīyamānaṁ na gṛhṇanti vinā mat-sevanaṁ janāḥ \|\| sa eva bhakti-yogākhya ātyantika udāhṛtaḥ \|\|”*

*ahaitukiī niṣkāmā, viśuddheti paryāyāḥ \| avyavahitā madhu-dhārāvan nirantarā yā bhakis tasyāṁ satyām; it śeṣaḥ \| mat seveanaṁ mamānandātmakasya bhakti-raskikasya ca prema-sevāṁ vinety arthaḥ \| sālokyādikaṁ tu pṛṣṭha-lagnam iveti tasyeha nābhilāsaḥ \|\|51\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This bhakti is ahaitukī, without other desires. This produces a direct vision of the Lord whereas other methods do not achieve this.**

**ahaituky avyavahitā yā bhaktiḥ puruṣottame \|\|**  
**sālokya-sārṣṭi-sāmīpya-sārūpyaikatvam apy uta \|**  
**dīyamānaṁ na gṛhṇanti vinā mat-sevanaṁ janāḥ \|\|**  
**sa eva bhakti-yogākhya ātyantika udāhṛtaḥ \|\|**

**These are the characteristics of transcendental loving service to Puruṣottama, the Supreme Lord. It is without other desires, and it is not contaminated by** ***karma*** **or** ***jñāna.*** **In this type of** ***bhakti*****, my devotees do not accept** ***sālokya, sārṣṭi, sārūpya, sāmīpya*** **or oneness with me—even if I offer these liberations—in preference to serving me. This is called** ***bhakti yoga*****, and is declared as the highest object of human pursuit. SB 3.29.12-14**

***Ahaitukī*** **means “without material desire or pure.”** ***Avyavahitā*** **means “continuous like a stream of honey.” In that bhakti, the devotees do not accept sālokya etc. even if offered, if there is no service in prema (*****mat-sevanam*****) to the Lord who is full of bliss, and who relishes bhakti. Sālokya etc. are accompanying results and the devotee does not desire them. \|\|51\|\|**

### Text 52

*nanu bhakter niṣkāmatvaṁ na sambhavati \| tat-prasāditena bhagavatā dīyamānasyānandasya kāmatvāt\| yathā bhāva-hāvādi-prasāditena kāntenārpyamāṇaṁ rata-sukhaṁ taruṇyā kāmyate tac ca kāntād anyad eveti evam eva śrutir api āha \| eṣa evānandayāti rasaṁ hy evāyaṁ labdhānandī bhavati \| iti cet maivam \| bhakteḥ phalaṁ ced bhajanīyād anyat syāt yathā kāntād anyat tad arpitaṁ rata-sukhaṁ tarhi sā saphalā bhavet \| prakṛte tu bhajanīyo bhagavān eva phalam ānandātmakatvāt \| ānando brahmeti vyajānād iti śruteḥ \| kiṁ ca kāntasyānanda-sādhamatvam eva \| bhagavatas tvānanda-rūpatvāt phala-rūpatvaṁ nirvivādam \| evaṁ ca puruṣārtha-mūrtitvātma-dātṛtvoktayo na vyākupyeyuḥ \| eṣa evānandayātīty atrātma-dāneneti rasam ity atra tu rasenaiva tena labdenānandī bhavatīti ca vyākheyam \|\| 52\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“It is not possible for bhakti to be without desire, since the devotee has desire for the bliss given by the Lord when the Lord shows mercy. Similarly, a young women desires the happiness of love, which is offered by a male lover who is pleased with her moods and gestures. That desire for love on the part of the woman is separate from (simply attaining) the male lover. This is explained by the śruti. Śruti says that the Lord gives bliss to the devotee and, attaining rasa, the devotee becomes blissful.”**

**This is not so. If the result of bhakti were different from the Lord who is the object of worship, it would be similar to saying that the joy of love that a woman obtains, offered by the man, is different from the man. But the Lord who is worshipped is the actual result, since he is full of bliss. Śruti says** ***ānanda brahmeti vyajānāt*****: one should know that Brahman is bliss. (Taittirīya Upaniṣad) Moreover (even in the material example) it is the man who produces the bliss for the woman. Since the Lord is the very form of bliss, he is also the very result of bhakti (bliss for the devotee).**

**syād etat \| bhakta-kṛtānukūlyād bhagavataḥ svarūpollāsa-rūpam sukhaṁ jala-kallolavat bhavatīti bhāṣitam ahi-kuṇḍalādhikaraṇe \| smṛtiś ca prītiḥ svayaṁ prītim agād gayasyeti \|**  
**“sakhyuḥ priyasya viprarṣer aṅga-saṅgāti-nirvṛtaḥ \| prīto vyamuñcad ab-bindūn**

### Text 53

*netrābhyāṁ puṣkarekṣaṇaḥ” (SB 10.80.19) tenānubhūtena sva-sukhollāsāt sakāmatvaṁ bhakteḥ prāptaṁ ghaṭṭa-kūdyām eva prabhātam iti cet mandam etat \| tenānubhūtena mat-sukhaṁ syād iti dhiṣaṇānudayāt śuddha-bhaktānām \| itarathā tesāṁ ahaitukītyādi-vākyānāṁ vyākopāpattiḥ \| tat sukham eva maṁ sukham iti niṣkāmatāṁ bhakter nāpahanti kintu puṣṇāty eva tām iti \|\|53\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Let that be. (Here is another argument.) From the favorable actions of the devotee arises the happiness of the Lord, whose very form is bliss. This is like water and waves. It is explained the adhikaraṇa called** ***ahikuṇdalavat*****.**  
**prītiḥ svayaṁ prītim agād gayasya**  
**The Lord, the life of the universe, the very form of satisfaction, became satisfied at the sacrifice of King Gaya. (SB 5.15.13)**

**sakhyuḥ priyasya viprarṣer**  
**aṅga-saṅgāti-nirvṛtaḥ**  
**prīto vyamuñcad ab-bindūn**  
**netrābhyāṁ puṣkarekṣaṇaḥ**

**The lotus-eyed Supreme Lord felt intense ecstasy upon touching the body of his dear friend, the wise brāhmaṇa, and thus he shed tears of love. SB 10.80.19**  
**Since the Lord feels bliss by his experience, it amounts to bhakti having a desire for happiness (since desiring the Lord means desiring bliss). It is simply like paying the taxes in the morning.”**

**This is a foolish argument. The mind of the person with pure bhakti does not think “I obtain my happiness by the Lord’s experience of happiness.” Otherwise statements about their causeless (ahaitukī) bhakti would be meaningless. The niṣkāma devotee, thinking, “The Lord’s happiness is my happiness” does not destroy pure bhakti but rather nourishes it. \|\|53\|\|**

### Text 54

*esā tu bhaktis tan-nitya-parikāra-gaṇād ārabhyedānīṁtaneṣv api tad bhakteṣu mandākinīva pracarati \| ataḥ tad bhakta-kṛpayaiva labhyeti śṛibhāgavatādisamvādaḥ \| tathā ca bhajanīyasyānanda-rūpatvād bhakter niṣkāmatvaṁ tad viṣayasya puruṣārtha-rūpatvaṁ ca siddham \|\|54\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Starting from the eternal associates of the Lord, this pure bhakti spreads to the present day devotees, like the Mandākinī River. Bhāgavatam says that this bhakti is obtained by the mercy of the devotees present in the world. Thus it is established that bhakti is niṣkāma and that the ultimate goal is the Lord, the object of worship, since the Lord the very form of bliss. \|\|54\|\|**

### Text 55

*nanv ānanda-rūpo bhagavān iti nopayuyate \| sītādi-vyasana-lālasena tena vaiṣayikānandasya samīhanāt \| tasmāt śabda-sparṣādi-visaya-sevayā vaiṣayikaṁ nāma sukham upajāyate \| tad eveha cāmutra ca svarga-śabda-vyapadeśyam ānanda iti veda-jñā manyante yasya lipsayā lokaḥ sarvām maryādām ākramya pravartate \| bhagavataḥ sukha-rūpatve tasya tal lālasā na syāt \| sarvasya ca lokasya tal lipsayā bhāvyam \| na caitad asti visayālābhāt tad anarhatvāc ca \| kecit paṅgv-andha-vadhirālasā-jarāturāś cānukṛta-vairāgyāḥ sukhino vayam iti rikta-bhāṣayā ajñān pratārayanti \| tasmād vaidikaiḥ śubha-karmabhir visayopalambhas tat sevayā sukhopapattir duḥkha-hānis tu taccheṣa-bhūta eva dīpa- prakāśasya timira-nivrttir iveti cet \|\|55\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“It is not proper to say that the Lord is bliss since the Lord as Rāma was eager for bliss of the senses through desire for Sītā. One obtains material happiness from service to the material sense objects. Those in knowledge of the Vedas consider that in this life and the next bliss is attainment of Svarga. Desiring that happiness, people ignore all sorts of rules. If the Lord were the very form of happiness, he should have no desire for such happiness. And if he were the form of happiness, all people would desire to attain the Lord. But one does not see people striving for the Lord. (Rather they pursue material objects for happiness.) And people do not attain material happiness from jñāna and are incapable of practicing vairāgya. Some persons who are lame, blind, deaf, lazy or afflicted with old age imitate renunciation of material enjoyment and by false statements, saying “We are happy,” simply cheat the ignorant. Therefore, by performing pious acts according to the Vedas, one attains material objects. By enjoying them, one attains happiness, and destruction of suffering is a secondary result. It is like the lamp destroying darkness.” (This is the opinion of Jaimini’s followers.) \|\|55\|\|**

### Text 56

*pāmaraṁ bravīṣi viṣaya-rasiko viśuddhānandānabhijñaḥ \| asti hi vaiṣayikātirikta-sukha-sampat \| sukham aham asvāpsaṁ na kiṇcid avediṣaṁ iti śruteḥ \| iha hi suṣupti-sukhaṁ nirvisāyaṁ pratīyate sārva-laukikaṁcaitat \| evaṁ ca jīvasyāmūla-bhūtasyājanya-sukha-rūpatve siddhe vibhor bhagavatas tu tādṛṣa-vipula-sukha-rūpatvaṁ sidhyati \| yo vai bhūmā tat-sukham iti ānando brahmeti vyajānād iti a śrutis tathāha \| kiṁ ca vaiṣayikāṁ nāma sukham indriyair viṣaya-grahe abhiniṣpadyate tad agrahe vinaśaytīti sarvānubhūtaṁ bhagavad-rūpaṁ sukhaṁ tu viṣaya-tyāge abhivyajyate tat-parigrahe tirobhavatīti \| nāvirato duścaritāt nāśaṇto nāsam āhitaḥ \| nāsāntamānasovāpi prajñānenainam āpnuyād ity ādi śruteḥ viṣayāviṣṭa-cittānāṁ viṣnvāveśaḥsudūrataḥ vāruṇādig gataṁ vastu brajan naindrīṁ kim āp nuyād iti smṛteś ca \|\|56\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**You speak foolishly. The enjoyer of material pleasure does not know about pure bliss. There is a wealth of happiness beyond material happiness. Śruti says:**  
**sukham aham asvāpsaṁ na kiṇcid avediṣaṁ**  
**I slept happily and was not aware of anything. Praśna Upaniṣad**

**In this experience, there is a type of happiness without sound, form or touch. It is a common experience for everyone. The unsuitable happiness of the jīva in this world has been established and the great happiness of the Lord has also been established.**  
**yo vai bhūmā tat-sukham**  
**The Lord (*****bhūmā)*** **is bliss. (Chāndogya Upaniṣad 7.23.1)**

**ānando brahmeti vyajānād**

**He understood that the Lord is bliss. Taittirīya Upaniṣad 3.2**

**Moreover, material pleasure is accomplished by perceiving sense objects. Not attaining those sense objects, the happiness is destroyed. This is everyone’s experience. But the happiness of the Lord’s form manifests with the rejection of material sense objects and disappears when material sense objects are perceived. Śruti says:**  
**A person who has not given up bad activities, who is disturbed, who has not controlled his senses, and who has not controlled his mind cannot attain the Lord by his knowledge. Kaṭha Upaniṣad 2.2.24**

**Smṛti says:**

**viṣayāviṣṭa-cittānāṁ viṣṇv-āveśaḥ sudūrataḥ \|**  
**vāruṇī-dig-gataṁ vastu vrajann aindrīṁ kim āpnuyāt \|\|**  
**For persons absorbed in material enjoyment absorption in Viṣṇu is most difficult. A person going east cannot catch an object going west. (Viṣṇu Purāṇa) \|\|56\|\|**

### Text 57

*vaiṣayikāṁ ca sukhaṁ tat-praticchavi-rūpam eveti \| śrutir āha etasyaivānandasyānyāni bhūtāni mātrām upajīvantīti \| ataeva sarvānanda-hetutvaṁ ca tasya siddham \| upekṣyaś ca viṣayānandaḥ \| “sukham aindriyakaṁ rājan svarge naraka eva ca \| dehināṁ yad yathā duḥkhaṁ tasmān neccheta tad budhaḥ” ityādi smrṭeḥ \| viṣayānandāya lokasya prayāsaḥ sarvāṁ maryādām ullaṅghya pravṛṭtiś ca yujyate anubhūtatvāt \| bhagavatas tu viśuddhānandasyāpy ananubhūtatvāt tasmai tām ullaṅghya pravrttir na sambhavati \| kathaṁcit anubhūte tu tasmin sarvaṁ parityajya sā dṛṣṭā yathā śri śukāmbrīṣādīnāṁ yathā ca kāsāṁcit vraja-subhruvām \|\|57\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Material happiness is a reflection of the Lord’s happiness. Śruti says:**

**etasyaivānandasyānyāni bhūtāni mātrām upajīvantīti \|**  
**All beings live because of that bliss. (Bṛhad-āraṇyaka Upaniṣad 4.3.32)**  
**Thus it is proved that the Lord’s is the cause of all bliss. Material happiness is to be disregarded. Smṛti says:**

**sukham aindriyakaṁ rājan svarge naraka eva ca \|**  
**dehināṁ yad yathā duḥkhaṁ tasmān neccheta tad budhaḥ**

**O King, the embodied living entity automatically experiences unhappiness in heaven or hell. Similarly, happiness will also be experienced, even without one's seeking it. Therefore a person of intelligent discrimination does not make any endeavor to obtain such material happiness. SB 11.8.1**

**The endeavor of all people for material happiness, overstepping all rules, and the attraction for that enjoyment, are suitable for people because they have experience of it. Because they have not experienced the pure bliss of the Lord, they are not attracted to the Lord’s bliss--to the extent that they go beyond all rules. If somehow they experience that bliss, they give up everything and become attracted to the Lord. Examples are Śukadeva, Ambāriṣa and some of the gopīs. \|\|57\|\|**

### Text 58

*nanu bhagavato ‘pi viṣaya-viśiṣṭatvāt tad-viṣaya-sevā-janyānandāya teṣāṁ tāsāṁ tathā pravṛttir iti cet mandam etat tad-viṣayāṇāṁ api svarūpānandānatirekāt \| tathā ca śrutiḥ “nehanānāstikiñcana “ ityādyā smṛtiś ca “ānanda-mātra-kara-pādamukhodarād” ity ādyā \| ataeva teṣāṁ atikṣobhakatvaṁ \| śuddha-bhaktānāṁ tattva-jñānām tatra niṣkāmā pravrṭtir ity abhihitam ahaitukyavyavahitā ity ādinā \| sukha-sādhane pravṛttiḥ sakāmā sukhe tu sā niṣkāmeti sarvābhyupagataṁ tasmād vipulānanda-rūpo bhagavān iti siddham \| īdṛṣo ‘pi sa bhakti-yantritaḥ puruṣārthatvāya prakāśāya vahnir iva mathita ity aveseyam \| sītādīnāṁ sukha-rūpa-śākti-vṛtti-bhūta-hlādinī-sāra-samaveta-samvit-sārātmakatvāt tad-vyasana-lālasenāpi na ko ‘pi doṣaḥ \|\|58\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**It is foolish to argue that because the Lord possesses excellent sense objects, the devotees are attracted to the bliss generated from contacting these sense objects since the sense objects are non-different from the bliss of the Lord’s svarūpa. Śruti says:**

**neha nānāsti kiñcana**  
**In the Lord, there is no distinction (of sense object and svarūpa of the Lord).**  
**Smṛti says:**  
***nirdoṣa-pūrṇa-guṇa-vigraha ātma-tantro***  
***niścetanātmaka-śarīra-guṇaiś ca hīnaḥ***  
***ānanda-mātra-kara-pāda-mukhodarādiḥ***  
***sarvatra ca svagata-bheda-vivarjitātmā***

**The Lord is a form with faultless, perfect qualities. He is independent. He is devoid of the qualities of the material body. His hands, feet, face and belly are all bliss. He is devoid of internal difference everywhere. (Nārada Pañcarātra)**

**Thus these (spiritual) elements attract the devotees. The pure devotees have knowledge of the true nature of the Lord. They are endowed with niṣkāma attraction as mentioned in the following:**

**nanv addhā mayi kurvanti kuśalāḥ svārtha-darśinaḥ**  
**ahaituky avyavahitāṁ bhaktim ātma-priye yathā**

**Certainly expert personalities, who can see their own true interest, render unmotivated and uninterrupted devotional service directly unto me, for I am most dear to the soul. SB 10.23.26**

**The tendency to acquire one’s own pleasure is sakāma and the tendency to give happiness to the Lord is niṣkāma. Everything has been confirmed. Thus it is established that the Lord is full of the greatest bliss. Though he has this nature, controlled by bhakti, he manifests himself as the goal of human endeavor just as fire is manifested from wood. And there is no fault if the Lord shows attachment to Sītā or other consorts, since they arise as the essence of his hlādinī and saṁvit śaktis, functions of his śakti of happiness. \|\|58\|\|**

### Text 59

*nanu pūrṇānandena bhagavatā bhaktais taj jñair arpyamāṇāḥ sevā gṛhyante iti nopayujyate pūṛānandatvādi virodhāt \| “naivātmanaḥ prabhur ayaṁ nija-lābha pūrṇo mānaṁ janād aviduṣaḥ kaaruṇo vṛnīte \| yad yaj jano bhagavate vidadhīta māṇaṁ tac cātmane pratimukhasya yathā mukahśrir iti cen mandam etat \|” “so śnute sarvān kāmān saha brahmaṇā vipaścitā” ”Bhoktāraṁ yajña-tapasāṁ sarva loka maheśvaram \| ahaṁ hi sarva-yajñānāṁ bhoktā ca prabhur eva ca” “patraṁ puṣpaṁ phalaṁ toyaṁ yo me bhaktyā prayaccati \| Tad ahaṁ bhakty upahṛtam aśmāmi prayatātmanaḥ” “yāḥ kriyāḥ saṁprayuktāḥ syur ekānta-gata-buddhibhiḥ \| tāḥ sarvāḥ śirasā devaḥ pratigṛhnāti vai svayam” āsu kila tad bhoktṛtva-śakti-bhaktārpita-sādara-grahaṇayor avagamāt svabhāvocita-sevāsu bhaktānāṁ pravṛttir upayuktā \| bhagavatā ca bhoktrā śreṣṭhānāṁ teṣāṁ tāḥ sādaraṁ grāhyāḥ \| naiveti vākyaṁ tv etad anukūlatayā neyam \|\|59\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“It is not proper that the Lord, who is full of bliss, accepts service offered by devotees who know the Lord, since this is contrary to the Lord being full of bliss already. It is said:**

**naivātmanaḥ prabhur ayaṁ nija-lābha-pūrṇo**  
**mānaṁ janād aviduṣaḥ karuṇo vṛṇīte**  
**yad yaj jano bhagavate vidadhīta mānaṁ**  
**tac cātmane prati- mukhasya yathā mukha-śrīḥ**

**The Lord, complete in himself and merciful, does not accept the respect of an ignorant person for his own benefit. Whatever that person gives to the Lord is for that person’s benefit, just as a face’s beauty produces a beautiful reflection. SB 7.9.11”**

**This is foolish since the śrutis and smṛtis also say the following:**

**so 'śnute sarvān kāmān saha brahmaṇā vipaścitā**

**He who knows that Brahman who is** ***satyam jñānam*** **enjoys all pleasures along with the all-knowing Brahman. Taittirīya Upaniṣad 2.1.2**

**bhoktāraṁ yajña-tapasāṁ sarva-loka-maheśvaram \|**  
**suhṛdaṁ sarva-bhūtānāṁ jñātvā māṁ śāntim ṛcchati \|\|**

**That** ***muni,*** **realizing me as the protector of sacrifice and austerities, the master of all controllers, and the protector of all living beings, then attains liberation. BG 5.29**

**patraṁ puṣpaṁ phalaṁ toyaṁ yo me bhaktyā prayacchati \|**  
**tad ahaṁ bhakty-upahṛtam aśnāmi prayatātmanaḥ \|\|**

**I accept what is offered with devotion by the unmotivated devotee—a leaf, flower, fruit or water. BG 9.26**

**yāḥ kriyāḥ saṁprayuktāḥ syur ekānta-gata-buddhibhiḥ**  
**tāḥ sarvāḥ śirasā devaḥ pratigṛhnāti vai svayam**

**The Lord accepts on his head all those actions offered by persons with unalloyed devotional minds. Mokṣa dharma**  
**Since it is understood from this that the Lord has the power to enjoy and that he accepts what is offered with love by the devotee, the tendency of the devotees to serve according to their nature is proper. The Lord, the enjoyer, must accept with love what is offered by the excellent devotees. One should make the statement quoted above (SB 7.9.11) favorable to this fact. \|\|59\|\|**

### Text 60

*nanv evaṁ bhoktus tasya bhogyālābhāt kvacit duḥkhaṁ apumarthaḥ prasajyeteti cet, mandam etat \| “manomayaḥ prāṇa-śarīro bhā-rūpaḥ satya-saṅkalpa ākāśātmā sarva-karmā sarva-kāmāḥ sarva-gandhaḥ sarva-rasaḥ sarvam idaṁ abhyāto avākyan ādaraḥ sa ātmāntar-hṛdaya etad brahma” ityādiṣu bhogyānāṁ nitya-siddha-śravaṇāt \| evaṁ ca pūrti-śrutir api nirvādhā itarathā bhogya-bhotṛtva-śakti-vaidhurya-krṭā tasmin na pūrtir āpatet \|\|60\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“This would mean that the Lord, the enjoyer would sometimes suffer when he cannot attain enjoyment. Therefore he should not be the goal.”**  
**This is foolish since it is said in scriptures that the Lord has enjoyable items eternally. It is said:**

**manomayaḥ prāṇa-śarīro bhā-rūpaḥ satya-saṅkalpa ākāśātmā sarva-karmā sarva-kāmāḥ sarva-gandhaḥ sarva-rasaḥ sarvam idaṁ abhyāto avākyan ādaraḥ.sa ātmāntar-hṛdaya etad brahma**

**That Brahman is understood by the mind (*****manomayaḥ*****). He is the controller of** ***prāṇa*****. He is effulgent, and his every desire is fulfilled. He is all pervading, performs attractive pastimes, and is endowed with all enjoyment, all fragrance and all rasa. He encompasses everything. He is silent. He is the support. That Brahman is in the heart. Chāndogya Upaniṣad 3.14.1-2**  
**The śruti statements concerning the completeness of the Lord are conclusive. If the Lord did not have enjoyable items and had no power to enjoy, it would mean that he is not complete. \|\|60\|\|**

### Text 61

*nanu vijaghatso ‘pipāsaḥ iti śrutya bhagavataḥ kṣu-tpipāsā pratiṣedāt nitya-tṛptena tena tāsāṁ sādara-graho na sambhaved iti cet ucyate tatra hi vāyu-vikāra-rūpa-prāṇābhāvāt tat-kāryayoṣ tayoḥ praiṣedho, natu bhoktṛtva-śakteś ca tasyāḥ so ‘śnute” ityādi śruit-smṛti-siddheḥ \| tathā ca yathā śrī-śuka-bhīṣmādheḥ saty api pauruṣe na smāra-vikāras tad vāsanābhāvāt, tathā bhoktur api tasya tādṛśa-prāṇābhāvāt tat-kārye te neti \| tasmāt nitya-tṛptasyāpi tasya bhakteccayāivea kṣut-pipase sveccā-mayasya ityādi smrteḥ \|*

*Vilīna-smāra-vikārasya puṁso vanitā-kaṭākṣa iva tad vikāra-prakāśaḥ \| bhakecchānusāriṇā satya-saṅkalpena tasya te prādurbhavetām \| loke tṛpto ‘pi prītyārpitaṁ bhuñjāno dṛśyate \| tasmād bhakto yadi icchati yad yac cārpayati tad eva tad upabhuṅkte tāvataivāsau tuṣyati nādhikena “bhakta-kṣaṇaḥ kṣaṇo viṣṇoḥ smṛtiḥ sevā sva-veśmani \| sva-bhogyasyārpaṇaṁ dānaṁ phalam indrādi-durlabham \|\|” iti smṛtiś ca \|\|61\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“The śruti says** ***vijighatso ‘pipāsaḥ*****: the Lord no hunger or thirst. (*****Chāndogya Upaniṣad*** **8.1.5) Since the Lord has no hunger or thirst, since he is eternally satisfied, it is not possible for the Lord to accept devotional offerings of the devotees.”**

**What is denied in the śruti statement is material hunger and thirst, since the Lord does not have material prāṇas, which are transformations of material air element. The scriptures do not deny that the Lord has power of enjoy since the śrutis and smṛtis also say** ***so ‘śnute*****: he enjoys. Though Śukadeva and Bhīṣma were males, they did not have lust since they did not have material impressions. Similarly the Lord, though an enjoyer, does not have material hunger or thirst since he has no material prāṇas. Though the Lord is fully satisfied and acts by his own desire, he displays hunger and thirst by the desire of the devotees. This is stated in the smṛtis. This is like a man who, though having subdued passion, manifests passion when a woman glances at him with desire. Hunger and thirst develop in the Lord since he follows the desire of the devotees, and he is satya-saṅkalpa (his purposes are all fulfilled). It is seen in this world that though a persons is full satisfied, he enjoys what is offered with affection. Thus if a devotee has a desire and offers things to the Lord, the Lord accepts those things and becomes very pleased with that, and has no other desire.**

***bhakta-kṣaṇaḥ kṣaṇo viṣṇoḥ smṛtiḥ sevā sva-veśmani \|***  
***sva-bhogyasyārpaṇaṁ dānaṁ phalam indrādi-durlabham \|\|***

**When the devotee is present, Viṣṇu is present. One serves the Lord by remembering him in one’s house. “Giving” means offering to the Lord what one will enjoy. The result of such giving cannot be attained even by Indra. Mahābhārata \|\|61\|\|**

### Text 62

*tadīyāni tu bhokṣāṇi svarūpa-śakti-rasātmakāny eva “śriyaḥ kāntāḥ kāntaḥ parama-puruṣaḥ kalpa-taravo drumā bhūmiś cintāmaṇi-gaṇa-mayi toyam amṛtam \| kathā gānaṁ nāṭyaṁ gamanam api vaṁśī priya-sakhi cid-ānandaṁ jyotiḥ param api tad āsvādyam api ca \|\|”*

*iti brahma-saṁhitāyāṁ “dravya-tattvaṁ śṛṇu brahman pravakṣyāmi samāsataḥ \|*

*sarva-bhoga-pradā yatra pādapāḥ kalpa-pādapāḥ \|\| bhavanti tādṛśā vallyas tad-bhavaṁ cāpi tādṛśam \| gandha-rūpaṁ svādu-rūpaṁ dravyaṁ puṣpādikaṁ ca yat \|\| heyāṁśānām abhāvāc ca rasa-rūpaṁ bhaved dhi tat \| tvag-bījaṁ caiva heyāṁśaṁ kaṭhināṁśaṁ ca yad bhavet \|\|*

*sarva tad bhautikaṁ viddhi na hy abhūtamayaṁ ca tat \| rasasya yogato brahman bhautikaṁ svāduvad bhavet \|\| tasmāt sādhyo raso brahman rasaḥ syād vyāpakaḥ paraḥ \| rasavad bhautikaṁ dravyam atra syād rasa-rūpakam \|\|” iti hayaśrīṣa-pañcarātre tathaiva pratīteḥ \|\|62\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord’s enjoyable items are filled with the rasa of his svarūpa-śakti:**

***śriyaḥ kāntāḥ kāntaḥ parama-puruṣaḥ kalpa-taravo***  
***drumā bhūmiś cintāmaṇi-gaṇa-mayi toyam amṛtam \|***  
***kathā gānaṁ nāṭyaṁ gamanam api vaṁśī priya-sakhi***  
***cid-ānandaṁ jyotiḥ param api tad āsvādyam api ca \|\|***
