**I worship this Goloka, where there are unlimited** ***gopīs*** **and their beloved Kṛṣṇa, the supreme form of God, where the trees and land fulfill all desires, where the water is as sweet as nectar, where the speaking is singing and the walking is dancing, where the flute is the best friend by announcing the joyful presence of Kṛṣṇa everywhere, where the sun and moon shine with knowledge and bliss eternally in perfect form, revealing all things, and where all relishable things are also knowledge and bliss. Brahma-saṁhitā**

**This is also seen in Hayaśīrṣa-pañcarātra:**

***dravya-tattvaṁ śṛṇu brahman pravakṣyāmi samāsataḥ \|***  
***sarva-bhoga-pradā yatra pādapāḥ kalpa-pādapāḥ \|\|***  
***bhavanti tādṛśā vallyas tad-bhavaṁ cāpi tādṛśam \|***  
***gandha-rūpaṁ svādu-rūpaṁ dravyaṁ puṣpādikaṁ ca yat \|\|***  
***heyāṁśānām abhāvāc ca rasa-rūpaṁ bhaved dhi tat \|***  
***tvag-bījaṁ caiva heyāṁśaṁ kaṭhināṁśaṁ ca yad bhavet \|\|***  
***sarva tad bhautikaṁ viddhi na hy abhūtamayaṁ ca tat \|***  
***rasasya yogato brahman bhautikaṁ svāduvad bhavet \|\|***  
***tasmāt sādhyo raso brahman rasaḥ syād vyāpakaḥ paraḥ \|***  
***rasavad bhautikaṁ dravyam atra syād rasa-rūpakam \|\|***

**Please hear about the nature of the objects there. I will explain it in brief. The trees are desire trees bestowing all enjoyment. The creepers are similar. There are many flowers and fruits, the essence of fragrance, taste and rasa since they have no inferior parts such as skin, seeds or hard sections. Know that all this is not material. It is most astonishing. Because of having rasa, Brahman appears full of taste like material objects. Brahman is ultimately rasa. That supreme rasa spreads everywhere. Because of spiritual rasa, material objects have rasa and are celebrated as tasty. Hayaśīrṣa-pañcarātra \|\|62\|\|**

### Text 63

*evam eva svīkṛtaṁ taj jñaiḥ “śruyate parimale mala-śabdo mekhalādiṣu khalādy-abhiyogaḥ*  
*candanādi-rasa eva hi paṅko nīvi-keśa-rasanādiṣu bandhaḥ” iit \|\|63\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This is accepted by the wise:**

**śruyate parimale mala-śabdo mekhalādiṣu khalādy-abhiyogaḥ**  
**candanādi-rasa eva hi paṅko nīvi-keśa-rasanādiṣu bandhaḥ**

**In Nanda’s kingdom, it is said that the word** ***mala*** **(contamination) means fragrance, the word** ***khala*** **(rascal) means a belt, the word** ***paṅka*** **(mud) means sandalwood pulp and the** ***bandha*** **(bondage) means the string which ties the dress or hair. Ānanda-vṛndāvana-cāmpu \|\|63\|\|**

### Text 64

*“evaṁ dhārṣtyāny uśati kurute mehanādīni vāstau steyāpāyair viracita-kṛtiḥ supratīko yathās” ity atra vāstau mehanādīni jala-seka-pāṅśu-prakṣepādīni dhārṣtyāni kurute ity evārthaḥ yad vā ameti sahārthe avyayam \| amā saha vasato ‘syāṁ candrārkāv ity amāvasyāpada-vyutpattau dṛṣṭatvāt amā saha samīpe ca iti nānārtha vargāc ca \| steyopāyair amā saha dhārstyāni kurute \| kīdṛṁśi nādīni kaṇṭhādi-nāda-vanti iti arthaḥ \|\|64\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**evaṁ dhārṣṭyāny uśati kurute mehanādīni vāstau**  
**steyopāyair viracita-kṛtiḥ supratīko yathāste**  
**itthaṁ strībhiḥ sa-bhaya-nayana-śrī-mukhālokinībhir**  
**vyākhyātārthā prahasita-mukhī na hy upālabdhum aicchat**

**When Kṛṣṇa is caught in his naughty activities, the master of the house will say to Him, ‘Oh, You are a thief,’ and artificially express anger at Kṛṣṇa. Kṛṣṇa will then reply, ‘I am not a thief. You are a thief.’ Sometimes, being angry, Kṛṣṇa passes urine and stool in a neat, clean place in our houses. But now, our dear friend Yaśodā, this expert thief is sitting before you like a very good boy.” Sometimes all the gopīs would look at Kṛṣṇa sitting there, His eyes fearful so that His mother would not chastise Him, and when they saw Kṛṣṇa’s beautiful face, instead of chastising him they would simply look upon his face and enjoy transcendental bliss. Mother Yaśodā would mildly smile at all this fun, and she would not want to chastise her blessed transcendental child. SB 10.8.31**

**The meaning here is that Kṛṣṇa performs bold activities like throwing water and dust (*****mehanādīni*****) in the houses (instead of urine and stool).**

### Text 65

*evam ṛsabhe bhagavati yā heyaṁśa-yogoktiḥ sā tv ajña-pratītānuvāda eva tat pratyetṛṇāṁ deva-māyā-vimohitā iti viśeṣaṇāt \| “idaṁ śarīraṁ mama durvibhāvyaṁ sattvaṁ hi me hṛdayaṁ yatra dharmaḥ \| pṛṣṭhe kṛto me yad adharma ārād ato hi mām ṛṣabhaṁ prāhur āryāḥ” tan-mūrteḥ para-tattva-rūpatvaṁ tenaiva kaṇṭhato ’bhihitam \| nahi tādṛśe tasmin asau sambhavet \| tat sevināṁ jīvānāṁ ca siddhānām atad yogaḥ smaryate “jagaj-jana-mala-dhvaṁsi-śravaṇa-smṛti-kīrtanām \| mala-mūtrādi-rahitāḥ puṇyaślokā” iti smrṭāḥ \|\|65\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Descriptions of material qualities in Ṛṣabha are mere explanations made by persons in ignorance. Such perceivers are bewildered by the Lord’s māyā.**

**idaṁ śarīraṁ mama durvibhāvyaṁ**  
**sattvaṁ hi me hṛdayaṁ yatra dharmaḥ**  
**pṛṣṭhe kṛto me yad adharma ārād**  
**ato hi mām ṛṣabhaṁ prāhur āryāḥ**

**My body is difficult to understand since it is spiritual. Where there is bhakti*****,*** **there my mind resides. Whatever is opposite to bhakti is behind me, far away. Thus the respectable people call me the best, or Ṛṣabha. (SB 5.5.19)**

**He himself explains that his form is spiritual substance. Inferior substance cannot exist in such a person. Even the jīvas who serve the Lord are devoid of inferior qualities. Smṛtis says:**

**jagaj-jana-mala-dhvaṁsi-śravaṇa-smṛti-kīrtanām**  
**mala-mūtrādi-rahitāḥ puṇyaślokā iti smrṭāḥ**

**The devotee are without urine and stool. Glorifying them, remembering them and hearing about themm destroys the impurities of all people. \|\|65\|\|**

### Text 66

*etasmād evam atra vyākheyam prāṇi-karmānurūpa-phala-prado bhagavān nāyaṁ deho deha-bhājām ityādisv okty upadiṣtā-dharma-niṣṭhān śiṣyān prati parataḥ mūrtitvenāvagataḥ prayacchati tebhyas tad abhīṣṭāṇi \| tad upadiṣṭa-dharṁa-bhrāṣṭāṇ pāmarān prati tu heyāṁśa –yogitayā pratītas tāṁs tān kriyānugunaṁ nakaraṁ prāpayatī iti mad dharma-vimukhā mām īdṛṣaṁ pratiyantv ācarantu ca tatheti tat kāme sati tan māyaiva tathā bhaṣayati \|\|66\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**From this, the following should be explained. The Lord who gives results according to the actions of the living being, gives whatever they desire to students who are fixed in the dharma that he teaches, as explained in words like “The** ***jīva*** **should not use his human body for enjoying objects (SB 5.5.1)” and who understand that his form is different from others. He makes those who have fallen from what he teaches, who think the Lord has inferior parts, go to hell, according to their actions. “Let those who, opposed to my dharma, disobey me, act as they choose”. Though the Lord desires in this way, his māyā makes such arrangements. \|\|66\|\|**

### Text 67

*heyasyāpy aheyatayāvabhānaṁ tadṛśācāra-jigrahiṣayaiveti mantavyam \| itarathā sva-dharma-deśikatvena tasmin āsthā na syāt\| yo hy adharmo bhagavatā pṛṣṭhe kṛtaḥ sa vaisa vaidika-śaucadi-viraha-deva-dvija-yajñādy-avajñāmayaḥ tair gṛhītaḥ \| muniḥ sphutāyati “yasya kilānucaritam upākarṇya koṅka-veṅka-kuṭakānāṁ rājārhan-nāmopaśikṣya kalāv adharma” (SB 5.6.9) tasmād ārhatān pāṣaṇḍinaḥ prati māyayedaṁ heyāṁśa-bhāsanaṁ na ca paramāpte bhagavati vaiṣamyam karmānusārini tasmins tad asambhavāt \|\|67\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One should understand that Ṛṣabha acted as he did, accepting what is detestable as acceptable, only to nourish such bad conduct among errant persons. Otherwise he would not have a position as a teacher of his path. What the Lord rejects as adharma, filled with rejection of the Lord, the dvija and sacrifice, and devoid of Vedic cleanliness, is accepted by these people. The sage Śukadeva makes this clear:**

**yasya kilānucaritam upākarṇya koṅka-veṅka-kuṭakānāṁ rājārhan-nāmopaśikṣya kalāv adharma**

**King Arhat of Koṇka, Veṅka and Kuṭaka regions, hearing about the activities of Ṛṣabhadeva, would become bewildered when** ***adharma*** **increased in Kali-yuga by the results of bad** ***karma.*** **(SB 5.6.9)**

**Thus it was not a fault that the supreme Lord adopted this repulsive appearance by māyā, for encouraging the Jain pāśāṇḍas. Fault is impossible for the Lord, who makes others follow karma. \|\|67\|\|**

### Text 68

*evaṁ sati tena saha dāvānalas tad vanaṁ dadāha ity atra tu kartṛ-sāhityām eva na karma-sāhityām svīkartavyam dāvanālaḥ vanaṁ dadāha ṛṣabhas tu tad vana-vāsinām avidyām iti yat tu ṛṣabhasya viṣnor evam anuśāsyātmajān ityādibhiḥ paramahaṁsa-dharmānuṣthānam uktaṁ tat khalu tad anukaraṇa-rūpam eva tathaiva pratīyateḥ \| sāmparāya-vidhir api pratītikyeva tāvatiava tad-āveśa-parikṣayāt \| tasmāt suṣthūktam heyāṁṣa-yogoktir ajña-pratītanuvāda eveti \| tasmāt svajñāna-pūrvakaṁ bhagavaj-jñanam evātyantika-duḥkha-nivṛtti-sukha-prāpty upāyabhūtaṁ bhavati. nāparaṁ kiṁcit asti \|\|68\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Though the statement** ***tena saha dāvānalas tad vanaṁ dadāha*** **appears to make Ṛṣabha an ordinary person, the meaning is otherwise. The real meaning is “The fire, along with Ṛṣabha, burned up the forest” rather than “The fire burned up the forest as well as Ṛṣabha.” The fire burned the forest and Ṛṣabha burned up the ignorance of the inhabitants of the forest.**

**Thus it is stated that Ṛṣabha, who was Viṣṇu, instructed his sons. (SB 5.5.28) His establishment of paramahaṁsa-dharma is described, but this is an imitation, an appearance only. His method of leaving his body is an appearance only, in order to test followers’ attachment to their bodies. The statement is true that seeing Ṛṣabha from a material view is due to ignorance.**

**Thus it is established that knowledge of the Lord along with knowledge of the self is the method for producing permanent destruction of suffering and attainment of permanent happiness. There is no other method. \|\|68\|\|**

## Chapter Two

**Defining the Powers of the Lord**

### Text 1

*athaiśvarya-rūpā mādhurya-rūpa ceti bhagavattāyā dvaividyāt tad-dvidhayor jñana-bhaktyor dvaividhyaṁ pradarśyate \| tatraiśvaryaṁ nara-līlām anapekṣya pāramaiśvaryāvirbhāvaḥ \| yathā pitarau praty aiśvaryaṁ pradarśya (SB 10.3.44) etadvāṁ darśitaṁ pūpaṁ prāg janma smaraṇāya me yathā (BG 11.8) cārjunaṁ p rati “paśya me yogam aiśvaryam” iti \| mādhuryam tu pāram aiśvaryasya prakāśe cāprakāśe ca nara-līlānatikramaḥ \| yatha pūtanā-prāṇa-hantṛtve stana-cūṣaṇa-rūpa-nara-bālaka-ceṣṭrtvaṁ \| cāti kathora-śakaṭotpāptane ‘py atikomalāghry adalam iti siddhatvam \| atidīrghadāmāśakyabandhe ‘pi mātṛbhīti-vaiklavyam \| brahmādi-mohane ‘py atisāra-jñe ‘pi vatsa-cāraṇa-līlatvaṁ \| pāramaiśvarye saty api tasyāprakāśanena dadhi-payaḥ steya-vraja-strī-jana-lolupatvādi \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord has two forms: a form of power and a form of sweetness. Two processes of realizing these are shown: jñāna and bhakti. The power means a manifestation of the Lord’s great powers, without regard for the Lord’s human-like pastimes. For example the Lord shows his powers to his parents:**

***etad vāṁ darśitaṁ rūpaṁ prāg-janma-smaraṇāya me***  
***nānyathā mad-bhavaṁ jñānaṁ martya-liṅgena jāyate***

**I have shown you this form of Viṣṇu just to remind you of my previous births. Otherwise, if I appeared like an ordinary human child, you would not believe that the Supreme Lord, Viṣṇu, has indeed appeared. SB 10.3.44**

**To Arjuna he also showed his powers:**

**na tu māṁ śakyase draṣṭum anenaiva sva-cakṣuṣā \|**  
**divyaṁ dadāmi te cakṣuḥ paśya me yogam aiśvaram \|\|8\|\|**

**But you will not be able to see this with your material eye alone. I give you the eye of the** ***devas*****. Behold my majestic form. BG 11.8**

**Sweetness however does not see anything except the human pastimes whether great powers are shown or not shown. For example, though the Lord kills Putanā, he remains in his human, baby form, sucking her breast. Though he overturned the hard cart, his feet remained very soft. Though Kṛṣṇa could not be tied up by a very long rope, he became overcome with fear of his mother. Though he bewildered Brahmā, and had all knowledge, he continued to herd the calves. Though Kṛṣṇa possesses all powers, he hides those powers and steals butter and shows desire for the gopīs. \|\|1\|\|**

### Text 2

*athaiśvarya-jñānaṁ pāramaiśvaryānusandhau sati hṛt-kampa-hetunā sambhrameṇa svabhāva-śaithilya-kṛd-dharma-viśeṣaḥ. Yathā yuvāṁ na naḥ sutau sākṣāt pradhāna-puruṣeśvarau (SB 10.85.18) iti yathā ca “sakheti matvā prasabhaṁ yad uktaṁ he kṛṣṇa he yādava he sakheti \| ajānatā mahimānaṁ tavedaṁ mayā pramādāt praṇayena vāpi \|\| yac cāvahāsārtham asat-kṛto ’si vihāra-śayyāsana-bhojaneṣu \| eko ’tha vāpy acyuta tat-samakṣaṁ tat kṣāmaye tvām aham aprameyam \|\|” (BG 11.41-42) iha vātsalya-sakhyātmaka-svabhāva-śaithilyaṁ \| madhuryā-jñānam tu pāramaiśvaryānusandhāne pi hṛt-kampaheu sambhramahetulavasyāpy anudayāt svabhāvātisthayirkrd dharma-veśesāḥ yatha “trayyā copaniṣadbhiś ca sāṅkhya-yogaiś ca sātvataiḥ \| upagīyamāna-māhātmyaṁ hariṁ sāmanyatātmajam \|vandinas tam upadeva-gaṇā ye vādya-gīta-balibhiḥ parivavruḥ \| vatsalo vraja-gavāṁ yad aga-dhro vandyamāna-caraṇaḥ pathi vṛddhaiḥ” ityādīha pāramaiśvaryānusandhāne pi vātsalyojjvalākhya-svabhāvāśaithilyam \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**On examining the great power of the Lord, realization of that power is a particular quality which restricts one’s nature by respect which is caused by throbbing of the heart (fear).**

**yuvāṁ na naḥ sutau sākṣāt**  
**pradhāna-puruṣeśvarau**  
**bhū-bhāra-kṣatra-kṣapaṇa**  
**avatīrṇau tathāttha ha**

**You are not our sons but the very Lords of both material nature and its creator \[Mahā-Viṣṇu\]. As you yourself have told us, you have descended to rid the earth of the rulers who are a heavy burden upon her. SB 10.85.18**

**sakheti matvā prasabhaṁ yad uktaṁ**  
**he kṛṣṇa he yādava he sakheti \|**  
**ajānatā mahimānaṁ tavedaṁ**  
**mayā pramādāt praṇayena vāpi \|\|**  
**yac cāvahāsārtham asat-kṛto ’si**  
**vihāra-śayyāsana-bhojaneṣu \|**  
**eko ’tha vāpy acyuta tat-samakṣaṁ**  
**tat kṣāmaye tvām aham aprameyam \|\|**

**I beg pardon from you, for not knowing your greatness. Through want of consideration or through affection, I looked upon you, the immeasurable, as a friend. I beg pardon for whatever I have said rudely, such as addressing you as “O Kṛṣṇa! O Yādava! O friend!” and for whatever disrespect I have shown to you in fun, while playing, lying, sitting together or dining, whether alone or in the company of others. BG 11.41.42**

**In these cases there is a restriction on the natures of vātsalya and sakhya. In realization of sweetness however, there is the quality of remaining fixed in one’s nature even if there is opportunity for recognizing the Lord’s great powers, since there is not even a trace of respect caused by throbbing of the heart (fear).**

**trayyā copaniṣadbhiś ca sāṅkhya-yogaiś ca sātvataiḥ**  
**upagīyamāna-māhātmyaṁ hariṁ sāmanyatātmajam**

**The glories of the Supreme Personality of Godhead are studied through the three Vedas, the Upaniṣads, the literature of Sāṅkhya-yoga, and other Vaiṣṇava literature, yet mother Yaśodā considered that Supreme Person her ordinary child. SB 10.8.45**

**vandinas tam upadeva-gaṇā ye vādya-gīta-balibhiḥ parivavruḥ**

**The various Upadevas, standing on all sides like panegyrists, offer their music, singing and gifts of tribute. SB 10.35.21**  
**vatsalo vraja-gavāṁ yad aga-dhro vandyamāna-caraṇaḥ pathi vṛddhaiḥ**  
**Exalted demigods standing along the path worship his lotus feet. SB 10.35.22**

**In these cases, though there are opportunities to realize the Lord’s great power (as illustrated in the last two verses quoted), there is no lessening of the natures of vātsalya and mādhurya (in the devotees). \|\|2\|\|**

### Text 3

*mādhurya-niṣṭhānām aiśvarya-jñānaṁ tri-veṇyāṁ sārasvata-pravāhavad gauṇatayāsti \| hṛt-kampa-hetoḥ sādara-sambhramasyābhāvāt \| na ca tad bhāva-saṅkoca-kṛt pratyuta tat poṣyeva \| mat suto mat-sakho mat-priyo vā sarveśvara ity ullasa-sambhavāt loke ‘pi yatha svaputrasya svasakhasya svakāntasya vā pṛthivīśvaratve sati tasmin vātsalyādaḥ puṣṭir bhavet tatheti draṣṭavyam \| vismaye virahe vipati ca taysodayaḥ parvaṇi sārasvatasyeva pravāhasya \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**For persons fixed in sweetness, knowledge of the Lord’s greatness remains a secondary feature, like the Sārasvatī River at the Triveṇī, since they have lack of awe and respect which is caused by fear. That knowledge does not restrict the devotees’ bhāva but rather nourishes it. The devotee becomes joyful, thinking, “My son, my friend or my lover is the Supreme Lord.” This is seen in this world as well. When one’s son, friend or lover becomes a king, the vātsalya, sakhya or madhurya increases. In astonishment, separation, and danger, such realization of Kṛṣṇa’s powers may arise (but this increases the vātsalya etc). It is like the increase of the Sārasvatī River during the full moon. \|\|3\|\|**

### Text 4

*aiśvaryavan mādhuryasyāpi brahma-dharmatvāt tad viṣayakam api jñānaṁ brahma-jñānam eva \| sahastaśīrṣatvādivat dvi-bhujatvāder api brahma-dharmatvaṁ śruti-smṛtibhyaḥ siddham \| sat puṇḍarīka-nayanaṁ vaidyutāmbaram \| dvibhujaṁ manua-mudrāḍyaṁ vanamālinam īśvaram \| (Gopāla Tāpanī Upaniṣad) yador vaṁśaṁ naraḥ śrutvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate \| āpanīyatrāvaitīrṇaṁ kṛṣṇākhyaṁ paraṁ brahma-narākṛtiḥ (SB 9.13.19-20) gūḍḥam paraṁ brahma manuyṣya-liṅgaṁ ityādibhyaḥ (SB 7.15.75) \| evaṁ cobhaya-jñāna-sattād brahmoddhavādi-vandya-padarajastvaṁ mādhurya-jñānavatāṁ smaryate \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Realization of the Lord’s sweetness is also brahma-jñāna, since, like realization of his powers, it is realization of the qualities of Brahman. The śrutis and smṛtis prove that the two armed form, like the thousand headed form, indicates the qualities of Brahman.**

**Meditating with concentration on two-armed lotus-eyed, silent Kṛṣṇa, who has the complexion of a cloud, who wears cloth flashing like lightning and a garland (one attains liberation). Gopāla-tāpanī Upaniṣad**

**varṇayāmi mahā-puṇyaṁ sarva-pāpa-haraṁ nṛṇām**  
**yador vaṁśaṁ naraḥ śrutvā sarva-pāpaiḥ pramucyate**  
**yatrāvatīrṇo bhagavān paramātmā narākṛtiḥ**

**I will now describe the dynasty of Yadu, the eldest son of Mahārāja Yayāti, which vanquishes all sins. Simply by hearing this description, one is freed from all sinful reactions. In that dynasty the Supreme Lord, Paramātmā, descended in his human form. SB 9.23.19-20**

**yūyaṁ nṛ-loke bata bhūri-bhāgā**  
**lokaṁ punānā munayo ’bhiyanti**  
**yeṣāṁ gṛhān āvasatīti sākṣād**  
**gūḍhaṁ paraṁ brahma manuṣya-liṅgam**

**You Pāṇḍavas are most fortunate in this world. Sages who purify the planets come to your houses because the Supreme Brahman personally resides in your houses in a human form. SB 7.15.75**

**Thus since both realization of powers and sweetness exist in these devotees, the dust from the lotus feet of devotees with mādhurya realization of Kṛṣṇa are worshiped by Brahmā, Uddhava and others. \|\|4\|\|**

### Text 5

*yat tu aiśvarya-jñāna-śūnyatvād eṣāṁ rāga-pradhānaṁ bhavad-upajīvyair abhihitaṁ tat kila hṛt-kampa-hetu-sādara-sambhramājanakatvād iti bodhyam na tv aiśvarya-jñāna-mātrābhāvāt taj-jñānasya darśitatvāt \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**It is said (by Rūpa Gosvāmī) that the people of Vraja have rāga predominantly, since they lack awareness of Kṛṣṇa’s powers. (But if they sometimes are aware of his powers, then is this awareness criticized?) One should understand that the people of Vrajam, even if they are aware of his powers, do not show respect and awe caused by fear. There is no complete forbiddance of awareness of Kṛṣṇa’s powers in the people, since Bhāgavatam gives instances where the inhabitants of Vraja realize his powers. \|\|5\|\|**

### Text 6

*tac ca “atho amusyaiva mamārbhakasya yaḥ kaścanautpattika ātmayogaḥ” “sarpo māṁ grāsate tāta prapannaṁ parimocaya” “dāvāgninā dahyamānān prapannāṁs trātum arhathaḥ” “mṛgayur iva kapīndraṁ vivyadhe lubdha-dharmā \| striyam akṛta virūpāṁ strī-jitaḥ kāma-yānām \| balim api balim attvāveṣṭayad dhvāṅkṣa-vad yas \| tad alam asita-sakhyair dustyajas tat-kathārthaḥ” varadeśvara māsma cchindyaḥ” Kintu tat taj jñānani krameṇodayante yugapat sarvāyogāt tathaiva rasa-poṣāc ca nyāpy anuditamasat pūmstvādivat tasya sato abhivyaktiyogāt” iti nyāyāt \| bhagavato ‘pi mihira-prakāsa-nyāyena nirantara-prakāśita-jñānasya manuṣya-līloyogi-saṁmugdhatvaṁ vīkṣyate \| “tad abhijño ‘pi bhagavān sarvātma sarvadarśanaḥ \| Praśrayā-vanato ‘ṛcchad vṛddhān nanda purogamān” “Tato vatsān” ity adau \| tac ca sva-ciccakti-sāra-rūpa-lilānandatmakām svarūpaāireki mantavyaṁ \| itarasya tasmin nabhāvāt “aṣṭa-dāśa-mahā-doṣai rahitā bhagavat tanuḥ sarvāśvaisvaryamayī satyavijñānandnarūpinī” iti smṛteḥ \| mahaḍoṣāś ca “Mohas tantrā bhramo rukṣarasaṭa kāma ulbaṇaḥ \| Lolaṭa mada-mātsarye himṣa kheda-paricṛamau \|\| asatyaṁ krodha ākāśkṣa āśaṅkā viśva-vibrhramaḥ \| viṣamatvam parāpekṣā doṣa aṣtādaśoditāḥ” iti \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**kiṁ svapna etad uta devamāyā kiṁ vā madīyo bata buddhi-mohaḥ**  
**atho amuṣyaiva mamārbhakasya yaḥ kaścanautpattika ātma-yogaḥ**

**\[Mother Yaśodā began to argue within herself:\] Is this a dream, or is it an illusory creation by the external energy? Has this been manifested by my own intelligence, or is it some mystic power of my child? SB 10.8.40**

**sa cukrośāhinā grastaḥ kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa mahān ayam**  
**sarpo māṁ grāsate tāta prapannaṁ parimocaya**

**In the clutches of the snake, Nanda Mahārāja cried out, “Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa, my dear boy! This huge serpent is swallowing me! Please save me, who am surrendered to you!” SB 10.34.6**

**kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa mahā-vīra he rāmāmogha vikrama**  
**dāvāgninā dahyamānān prapannāṁs trātum arhathaḥ**

**\[The cowherd boys said:\] O Kṛṣṇa ! Kṛṣṇa! Most powerful one! O Rāma! You whose prowess never fails! Please save your devotees, who are about to be burned by this forest fire and have come to take shelter of you! SB 10.19.9**

**mṛgayur iva kapīndraṁ vivyadhe lubdha-dharmā**  
**striyam akṛta virūpāṁ strī-jitaḥ kāma-yānām**  
**balim api balim attvāveṣṭayad dhvāṅkṣa-vad yas**  
**tad alam asita-sakhyair dustyajas tat-kathārthaḥ**

**Like a hunter, he cruelly shot the king of the monkeys with arrows. Because He was conquered by a woman, He disfigured another woman who came to him with lusty desires. And even after consuming the gifts of Bali Mahārāja, He bound him up with ropes as if he were a crow. So let us give up all friendship with this dark-complexioned boy, even if we can’t give up talking about him. SB 10.47.17**

**kurvanti hi tvayi ratiṁ kuśalāḥ sva ātman**  
**nitya-priye pati-sutādibhir ārti-daiḥ kim**  
**tan naḥ prasīda parameśvara mā sma chindyā**  
**āśāṁ dhṛtāṁ tvayi cirād aravinda-netra**

**Expert transcendentalists always direct their affection toward you because they recognize you as their true self and eternal beloved. What use do we have for these husbands, children and relatives of ours, who simply give us trouble? Therefore, O supreme controller grant us your mercy. O lotus-eyed one, please do not cut down our long-cherished hope to have your association. SB 10.29.33**

**The two awarenesses arise at different times, since it would not be suitable simultaneously. Thus they nourish rasa. That does not mean that the awareness does not exist when not manifested.**

**You cannot say that the jīva's knowledge is not eternal, because it exists during deep sleep and simply manifests on waking. It is like maleness which is unmanifest in a child but appears when he grows up. Vedānta-sūtra 2.3.29**

**Though the Lord has eternal knowledge, it is seen that even he becomes absorbed in his human pastimes, just as the sun appears to be covered by clouds.**

**tad-abhijño 'pi bhagavān sarvātmā sarva-darśanaḥ**  
**praśrayāvanato 'pṛcchad vṛddhān nanda-purogamān**

**Being the omniscient Supersoul, the Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa already understood the situation, yet he still humbly inquired from the elders, headed by his father, Nanda Mahārāja. SB 10.24.2**

**tato vatsān adṛṣṭvaitya puline 'pi ca vatsapān**  
**ubhāv api vane kṛṣṇo vicikāya samantataḥ**

**Thereafter, when Kṛṣṇa was unable to find the calves, he returned to the bank of the river, but there he was also unable to see the cowherd boys. Thus he began to search for both the calves and the boys, as if he could not understand what had happened. SB 10.13.16**

**One should understand that Kṛṣṇa at these times is filled with the bliss of pastimes, which is the essence of his cit-śakti and non-different from his svarūpa. Real ignorance cannot exist in him.**

**aṣṭādaśa-mahā-doṣai rahitā bhagavat-tanuḥ \|**  
**sarvaiśvaryamayī satya-vijñānānanda-rūpiṇī \|\|**

**The form of the Lord is without the eighteen great faults, is endowed with all powers and is the very essence (svarūpa) of existence, knowledge and bliss.**

**mohas tandrā bhramo rukṣa-rasatā kāma ulbaṇaḥ \|**  
**lolatā mada-mātsarye hiṁsā kheda-pariśramau \|\|**  
**asatyaṁ krodha ākāṅkṣā āśaṅkā viśva-vibhramaḥ \|**  
**viṣamatvaṁ parāpekṣā doṣā aṣṭādaśoditāḥ \|\|**

**The eighteen faults are bewilderment, sleep, error, material attachment without** ***prema*****, material lust which brings suffering, fickleness, intoxication, envy, violence, exhaustion, toil, lying, anger, hankering, worry, absorption in worldly affairs, prejudice, and dependency on others. Viṣṇu-yamala \|\|6\|\|**

### Text 7

*nanu tato vaṁ sānadṛṣṭvaitya puline ‘pi ca vatsapān \| ubhāv api van kṛṣṇo vicikāy samantataḥ ity atra mohaḥ kvacti pallava-palpeṣu niyuddhaśramakarṣitaḥ \| vkṛṣa-mūlāśaryaaḥ śete gopotsaṅgopabarhaṇaḥ ity atra tandrākheda-śramāḥ. Tāv aṅghri-yugmam ityādau mugdha-prabhītavad upeyaturantimātroḥ iti bhramaḥ vatsān muñcan kvacid asamaye kroṣa-sañjātahāsaḥ ithādau lolatā; mad-vighūrṇitalocana iṣanmānadaḥ sva-suhrḍāṁ vanamālī ityādau madaḥ; lokeśamānināṁ lauḍhyād dhariṣhe śrīmadaṁ ṭamaḥ ityādau mātsaryaṁ; himsā ca pūtanādivadaḥ ; nāhaṁ bhakṣitavānamba sarve mithyābhiśaṁsinaḥ ityatra jarāsandha cchaladau cāsatyaṁ ; krodho ‘pi tatra tara prasiddha eva \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**His mohā (bewilderment) is illustrated as follows:**

**tato vatsān adṛṣṭvaitya puline 'pi ca vatsapān**  
**ubhāv api vane kṛṣṇo vicikāya samantataḥ**

**Thereafter, when Kṛṣṇa was unable to find the calves, he returned to the bank of the river, but there he was also unable to see the cowherd boys. Thus he began to search for both the calves and the boys, as if he could not understand what had happened. SB 10.13.16**

**Laziness, exhaustion and fatigue are illustrated:**

**kvacit pallava-talpeṣu niyuddha-śrama-karśitaḥ**  
**vṛkṣa-mūlāśrayaḥ śete gopotsaṅgopabarhaṇaḥ**

**Sometimes Lord Kṛṣṇa grew tired from fighting and lay down at the base of a tree, resting upon a bed made of soft twigs and buds and using the lap of a cowherd friend as His pillow. SB 10.15.16**

**Making mistakes is illustrated:**

**tāv aṅghri-yugmam anukṛṣya sarīsṛpantau**  
**ghoṣa-praghoṣa-ruciraṁ vraja-kardameṣu**  
**tan-nāda-hṛṣṭa-manasāv anusṛtya lokaṁ**  
**mugdha-prabhītavad upeyatur anti mātroḥ**

**When Kṛṣṇa and Balarāma, with the strength of their legs, crawled in the muddy places created in Vraja by cow dung and cow urine, their crawling resembled the crawling of serpents, and the sound of their ankle bells was very charming. Very much pleased by the sound of other people's ankle bells, They used to follow these people as if going to their mothers, but when they saw that these were other people, they became afraid and returned to Their real mothers, Yaśodā and Rohiṇī. SB 10.8.22**

**His greed is illustrated:**

**vatsān muñcan kvacid asamaye krośa-sañjāta-hāsaḥ**  
**steyaṁ svādv atty atha dadhi-payaḥ kalpitaiḥ steya-yogaiḥ**  
**markān bhokṣyan vibhajati sa cen nātti bhāṇḍaṁ bhinnatti**  
**dravyālābhe sagṛha-kupito yāty upakrośya tokān**

**Our dear friend Yaśodā, your son sometimes comes to our houses before the milking of the cows and releases the calves, and when the master of the house becomes angry, your son merely smiles. Sometimes He devises some process by which He steals palatable curd, butter and milk, which He then eats and drinks. When the monkeys assemble, He divides it with them, and when the monkeys have their bellies so full that they won't take more, He breaks the pots. Sometimes, if He gets no opportunity to steal butter or milk from a house, He will be angry at the householders, and for His revenge He will agitate the small children by pinching them. Then, when the children begin crying, Kṛṣṇa will go away. SB 10.8.29**

**Intoxication is illustrated:**

**mada-vighūrṇita-locana īṣat māna-daḥ sva-suhṛdāṁ vana-mālī**

**As Kṛṣṇa respectfully greets his well-wishing friends, His eyes roll slightly as if from intoxication. He wears a flower garland. SB 10.35.24**

**Envy is illustrated:**

**tatra pratividhiṁ samyag ātma-yogena sādhaye**  
**lokeśa-mānināṁ mauḍhyād dhaniṣye śrī-madaṁ tamaḥ**

**By my mystic power I will completely counteract this disturbance caused by Indra. Demigods like Indra are proud of their opulence, and out of foolishness they falsely consider themselves the Lord of the universe. I will now destroy such ignorance. SB 10.25.16**

**Violence is illustrated in killing Pūtanā and others. Lying is illustrated in cheating Jarāsandha and others.**

**nāhaṁ bhakṣitavān amba sarve mithyābhiśaṁsinaḥ**  
**yadi satya-giras tarhi samakṣaṁ paśya me mukham**

**My dear mother, I have never eaten dirt. All my friends complaining against me are liars. If you think they are being truthful, you can directly look into my mouth and examine it. SB 10.8.35**

**His anger is also seen in various pastimes. \|\|7\|\|**

### Text 8

*“tāṁ stanya-kāma āsādya mathnantīṁ jananīṁ hariḥ \| gṛhītvā dadhi-manthānaṁ nyaṣedhat prītam āvahan \|\|” ity atra ākāṅkṣā; “kvāpy adṛṣtvāntar vipine vatsān pālāṁ śa viśvavit \| sarvaṁ vidhi-kṛtaṁ kṛṣṇaḥ sahasāvajagāma ha” ity atra āsāṅkā ; “so ‘kāmayata bahu syāṁ prajāyeya ity ādau jagad-āveśa-rūpo viśva-vibhramaḥ samo ‘ham sarvabhūteṣu na me dveṣyo ‘sti na priyaḥ ye bhajanti to māṁ bhaktyā mayi te teṣu capy aham” ity ādau vaiṣamyaṁ; “ahaṁ bhakta-parādhīnaḥ” ity ādau parāpekṣā cāvagamyate \| rukṣa-rasatā prema-sambandhādrte rāgaḥ; ulbaṇo duḥkha-hetuḥ kāmaḥ; tāv etau māstām tataś ca mohādīnām ṣoḍaśānāṁ pramāṇa-siddhatvān nirdoṣatantvaṁ katham iti cet na bhaktānanda-vaicitrya-poṣaka-līlā-vilāsa-bhakta-saṁrakṣaṇa-bhakta-vātsalyādi-siddhaye prākṛta-gandhāspṛṣṭāḥ svarūpa-dharmā evaite udayante tān vinā lilādy asiddheḥ \| tad asiddau ca pūrṇatvānupapattiḥ \| itireṣu ca sarveṣu guṇeṣu rucyabhāvāt tad bhakti-anupapattiḥ \| tataś ca “śraddhā-bhakti-dhyāna-yogād avehi” ityādi śruti-vyākopāpattir iti \| teṣāṁ guṇatvād eva nirḍoṣa pūrṇa-guṇeṇetyādi smṛtiḥ saṁgacchate \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**His desire is illustrated:**

**tāṁ stanya-kāma āsādya mathnantīṁ jananīṁ hariḥ**  
**gṛhītvā dadhi-manthānaṁ nyaṣedhat prītim āvahan**

**While mother Yaśodā was churning butter, Lord Kṛṣṇa, desiring to drink the milk of her breast, appeared before her, and in order to increase her transcendental pleasure, He caught hold of the churning rod and began to prevent her from churning. SB 10.9.4**

**Worry is illustrated:**

**kvāpy adṛṣṭvāntar-vipine vatsān pālāṁś ca viśva-vit**  
**sarvaṁ vidhi-kṛtaṁ kṛṣṇaḥ sahasāvajagāma ha**

**When Kṛṣṇa was unable to find the calves and their caretakers, the cowherd boys, anywhere in the forest, he could suddenly understand that this was the work of Lord Brahmā.SB 10.13.17**

**Absorption in worldly affairs, in creating the universe, is illustrated:**

**so ‘kāmayata bahu syāṁ prajāyeya**  
**Let there be many. May I produce progeny! Taittirīya Upaniṣad**

**Prejudice is illustrated:**

**samo ’haṁ sarva-bhūteṣu na me dveṣyo ’sti na priyaḥ \|**  
**ye bhajanti tu māṁ bhaktyā mayi te teṣu cāpy aham \|\|**

**I am equal to all living beings. I do not hate anyone nor do I favor anyone. But those who worship me with devotion are in me, and I am in them. BG 9.29**

**Dependence on others is illustrated:**

**ahaṁ bhakta-parādhīno hy asvatantra iva dvija**  
**sādhubhir grasta-hṛdayo bhaktair bhakta-jana-priyaḥ**

**I am completely under the control of my devotees. Indeed, I am not at all independent. Because my devotees are completely devoid of material desires, I sit only within the cores of their hearts. What to speak of my devotee, even those who are devotees of my devotee are very dear to me. SB 9.4.63**

***Rukṣa-rasatā*** **means attachment without prema.** ***Ulbaṇa*** **means lust which brings suffering. These two should not be present in the Lord. “How can the Lord be without fault when evidence has been given for sixteen of the eighteen faults in the Lord?”**

**For accomplishing bliss in the devotees, for pastimes which nourish variety, for protecting devotees and for showing affection to the devotees, these qualities, untouched completely by matter, manifest as aspects of the Lord’s svarūpa. Without them, the pastimes could not be accomplished. And if they were not accomplished, it would mean the Lord is incomplete. Bhakti itself would not be accomplished since there would be lack of taste in all other qualities (like omniscience etc.). This would also contradict śruti statements such as the following:**

**śraddhā-bhakti-dhyāna-yogād avehi**

**One brings the Lord close by faith, bhakti and meditation. Kaivalya Upaniṣad 2**  
**This agrees with smṛti statements which describe the Lord as having all qualities, but without fault. \|\|8\|\|**

### Text 9

*tatra te maugdhyādayaḥ svarūpa-śakti-sāra-bhūta-līlānandātmaka-prema-svarūpānatireki-nakhara-kuntalādikavat tasmin mantavyāḥ \| “kva śoka-mohau krodho vā bhayaṁ ye aṅga-sambhavāḥ \| kva cākhaṇḍita-vijñāna-jñāṇaiśvaryāḥ sureḍitaḥ” ity atra ye ‘jña-sambhavās te cetty ukter buddhi-sambhavās te na santīti matatvāt \| “tat smāritānsuhṛtākhilendriyāḥ” ity ādau vibhvasta-tamasām śrī śukādīnāṁ ca bhagavat-prema-moho dṛṣṭaḥ \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**One should accept that these qualities are nondifferent from Lord’s prema-svarūpa, full of pastimes which are non-different from the essence of his svarūpa-śakti, like his finger nails and locks of hair (usually considered to be impure).**

**kva śoka-mohau sneho vā bhayaṁ vā ye 'jña-sambhavāḥ**  
**kva cākhaṇḍita-vijñāna-jñānaiśvaryas tv akhaṇḍitaḥ**

**How can lamentation, bewilderment, material affection or fear, all born out of ignorance, be ascribed to the infinite Supreme Lord, whose perception, knowledge and power are all similarly infinite? SB 10.77.31**

**In material persons, lamentation and bewilderment arise in people out of ignorance (*****ajña-sambhavaḥ)*****. Those qualities do not arise from intelligence. One sees qualities such as bewilderment from the prema in devotees like Śukadeva, who are free of ignorance.**

**itthaṁ sma pṛṣṭaḥ sa tu bādarāyaṇis**  
**tat-smāritānanta-hṛtākhilendriyaḥ**  
**kṛcchrāt punar labdha-bahir-dṛśiḥ śanaiḥ**  
**pratyāha taṁ bhāgavatottamottama**

**O Śaunaka, greatest of saints and devotees, when Mahārāja Parīkṣit inquired from Śukadeva Gosvāmī in this way, Śukadeva Gosvāmī, immediately remembering subject matters about Kṛṣṇa within the core of his heart, externally lost contact with the actions of his senses. Thereafter, with great difficulty, he revived his external sensory perception and began to speak to Mahārāja Parīkṣit about kṛṣṇa-kathā. SB 10.12.44 \|\|9\|\|**

### Text 10

*“gopy ādade tvayi kṛtāgasi dāma tāvad yā te daśāśru-kalilāñjana-sambhramākṣam \| vaktraṁ ninīya bhaya-bhāvanayā sthitasya sā māṁ vimohayati bhīr api yad bibheti \|\|” “mantreṣu māṁ vā upahūya yat tvam akuṇṭhitākhaṇḍa-sadātma-bodhaḥ \| pṛccheḥ prabho mugdha ivāpramattas tan no mano mohayatīva deva \|\|” ity ādiṣu tattva-vidām api śrī-śuka-uddhavādīnāṁ moha-darśanān nānukṛti-rūpās te śrī-śukādibhir āveśyoktatvāc ca \| loka viḍambanādi-śabdas tv atrāpi divya-līlāyā lokācārātikramāt saṁgacchate \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**There are verses such as the following:**

**mantreṣu māṁ vā upahūya yat tvam**  
**akuṇṭhitākhaṇḍa-sadātma-bodhaḥ**  
**pṛccheḥ prabho mugdha ivāpramattas**  
**tan no mano mohayatīva deva**

**O my Lord, your eternal Self is never divided by the influence of time, and there is no limitation to your perfect knowledge. Thus you were sufficiently able to consult with yourself, yet you called upon me for consultation, as if bewildered, although you are never bewildered. And this act of yours bewilders me. SB 3.4.17**

**gopy ādade tvayi kṛtāgasi dāma tāvad**  
**yā te daśāśru-kalilāñjana-sambhramākṣam**  
**vaktraṁ ninīya bhaya-bhāvanayā sthitasya**  
**sā māṁ vimohayati bhīr api yad bibheti**

**My dear Kṛṣṇa, Yaśodā took up a rope to bind you when you committed an offense, and your perturbed eyes overflooded with tears, which washed the mascara from your eyes. And you were afraid, though fear personified is afraid of you. This sight is bewildering to me. SB 1.8.31**

**In such statements, the bewilderment described by knowers of truth such as Śukadeva and Uddhava does not mean that the Lord was merely imitating qualities like bewilderment. The qualities manifested because of the Lord’s absorption in the pastimes. Being spiritual pastimes, they are beyond the conduct of human beings. That is the meaning of words like** ***loka-viḍambana*** **(fooling people). \|\|10\|\|**

### Text 11

*tad eva maugdhya-sārvajñayor guṇayoḥ sahāvirbhāvāt sva-sevakārti-stuti-paricaryādy-avadhānam api siddham \| evaṁ coktam anubhavitṛbhiḥ ---“sarvajñatve ca maugdhye ca sārvabhaumam idam mahaḥ \| nirviśan nayanaṁ hanta nirvāṇa-padam aśnute” iti \| viruddhānām guṇānāṁ sahāvasthitih smaryate “aiśvarya-yogād bhagavān viruddhārtho ‘bhidhīyate \| tathāpi doṣāh parame naivāhāryāḥ kathaṁcana guṇa-viruddhā apy ete samāhāryāḥ samantataḥ” iti \| tad idam abhithitaṁ bhagavatā suṭrakāreṇa “sarvadharmopapatteś ca” iti \| parameśvaraḥ khalu śabdaikanirṇeya yāthātmyo bhavati na tu tarkais tatra parividhānaṁ yujyata iti coktam “śrutes tu śabda-mūlatvāt” iti \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Since the Lord simultaneously is ignorant (in some pastimes) and is all knowing, he is attentive to his devotees’ suffering, praises and services. A realized soul has said:**  
**The smṛtis state that contrary qualities exist together in the Lord.**

**aiśvarya-yogād bhagavān viruddhārtho’bhidhīyate \|**  
**tathāpi doṣāḥ parame naivāhāryaḥ kathañcana \|**  
**guṇā viruddhā api tu samāhāryāś ca sarvataḥ \|\|**

**Because of his endowment of powers, Bhagavān is said to have contrary characteristics. Still, the fault of birth and transformation can never be found in the Supreme Lord. But contrary qualities are found together in the Lord. (Kūrma Purāṇa)**

**This is also stated by the Lord in Brahma-sūtras:**  
**dharmopapatteś ca**

**Bhūmā refers to Viṣṇu, because of the evidence--qualities appropriate to Viṣṇu are mentioned in the text. Vedānta-sūtra 1.3.9**

**The supreme Lord is to be determined as he is only by scriptures and not by logic. This is the arrangement made. The Lord also says:**  
**śrutes tu śabda-mūlatvāt**

**There is no defect because scripture declares it and scripture is the root of all knowledge. (BS 2.1.17)**

**\|\|11\|\|**

### Text 12

*anye tv evam āhuḥ -- maugdhyādayaḥ prapañca-dharmā bhagavatānukriyante svajanānandāya “prapañcaṁ niṣprapañco ‘pi viḍambayasi bhūtale \| prapanna-janatānanda-sandhoahṁ prathituṁ prabho” ityādi smṛteḥ \| tathāpi tattva-vidāṁ svaysa ca tai rasodayaḥ syāt lavaṇākara-nipāta-nyāyāt tat-kṛtatvena teṣv acintyatva-prāpteḥ \| Śrībala-pradyumnādi-rūpeṇa śiṣyatva-bhṛtyatvādy-anukāro hi dṛṣṭaḥ \| devarṣi-rūpeṇa bhaktavtādy anukāraś ceti \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Others also say that the Lord manifests qualities like ignorance for giving bliss to his devotees:**

**prapañcaṁ niṣprapañco ’pi viḍambayasi bhū-tale**  
**prapanna-janatānanda-sandohaṁ prathituṁ prabho**

**My dear master, although you have nothing to do with material existence, you come to this earth and imitate material life just to expand the varieties of ecstatic enjoyment for your surrendered devotees. SB 10.14.37**

**Rasa (bliss) arises by these qualities for both the Lord and the knowers of the truth, just as the salt ocean makes everything that enters it salty. These qualities attain an inconceivable nature by the Lord’s arrangement. One sees that the Lord takes the role of a student or servant when he assumes the forms of Bāladeva, Pradyumna and others. As Nārada, he takes the role of a devotee. \|\|12\|\|**

### Text 13

*tad evam aiśvaryā-mādhurya-viṣayatvena bhagavato jñānaṁ dvidhā darśitaṁ \| tena tad dvārikāyā bhakteś ca dvaividhyena bhāvyam \| tatrādyā vidhi-bhaktiḥ : tayā siddhāḥ; sva-niṣṭhādayaś cārccirādi-vartmanā paraṁ padaṁ labhante \| “te ‘rcciṣam” ity ādi śruteḥ \| ye tv ārtās tad-adhika-kṛpā-pātrās te khalu bhagavataiva tārkṣyavāhanena tad viśantīti mantavyaṁ vārāha-caramāt \| yathāha bhudeviṁ prait bhavān varāhadevaḥ “so ‘ham āśrita-mantrātmā smarāmi ca tad āśrayam \| jñāna-vairāgya-bhaktyādi-kriyā-rūpa-prakāśanāt \| nayāmi paramaṁ sthānam arccirādi gatiṁ vinā \| garuḍa-skandham āropya yatheṣṭam anivāritaḥ \|\| iti \| etān udiṣyāha pārthasārathiḥ “ ye tu sarvāni karmāṇi mayi sannyasya mat parāḥ \| ananyenaiva yogena māṁ dhyayanta upāsate \|\| teṣāṁ ahaṁ samuddhartā mṛtyu-saṁsāra-sagarāt \| bhavāmi na cirāt partha may āveśita-cetasām” iti \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Twp types of knowledge of the Lord, making him the object of power and of sweetness, have been described. One should understand that there are two types of bhakti for those realizations. The first is vidhi-bhakti. Those who attain perfection by vidhi-bhakti, sva-niṣṭhas, attain the supreme abode by the path of the sun. Śruti says:**

***arciṣam abhisambhavanti***

**Persons who know the person in the eye go by the path of light (and attain liberation). (Chāndogya Upaniṣad 4.15.1)**

**But those eager to attain the Lord, who have been objects of greater mercy of the Lord, enter the spiritual world by the Lord, being carried by Garuḍa. This is descrbed at the end of the Varāha Purāṇa. Varāha says to the earth:**

**so ‘ham āśrita-mantrātmā smarāmi ca tad āśrayam \|**  
**jñāna-vairāgya-bhaktyādi-kriyā-rūpa-prakāśanāt \|**  
**nayāmi paramaṁ sthānam arccirādi gatiṁ vinā \|**  
**garuḍa-skandham āropya yatheṣṭam anivāritaḥ**

**Being the very form of the mantra which the devotee chants, I remember that devotee.**

**Putting the devotee on the shoulders of Garuḍa, by my will, without obstacles, I take the devotee to the supreme abode without going by the path of light because the person has performed jñānā, vairāgya and bhakti. Varāha Purāṇa**

**The Lord says in Gītā:**

**ye tu sarvāṇi karmāṇi mayi sannyasya mat-parāḥ \|**  
**ananyenaiva yogena māṁ dhyāyanta upāsate \|\|6\|\|**  
**teṣām ahaṁ samuddhartā mṛtyu-saṁsāra-sāgarāt \|**  
**bhavāmi na cirāt pārtha mayy āveśita-cetasām \|\|7\|\|**

**But for those who, having surrendered to me, having given up all activities in order to attain me, meditate on me and worship me with ananya-bhakti-yoga, I quickly become the deliverer from the ocean of repeated birth and death, O son of Pṛthā. BG 12.6.7 \|\|13\|\|**

### Text 14

*antimā ruci-bhakti-rūpā tayā siddhās tu svayaṁ bhagavataiva tad viśantīti tac ca ramādi-labdhakaimutyād abhyupeyam “ nāyaṁ sukhāpaḥ” ityādeś ca \| ete tu tasyātipreṣṭhāḥ “ tad bhaveṣu tad rūci-nirbharāt ; yathāha govardhanoddharaṇativismitair gopaihr devadi- bhavenāśaṅkito \| bhagavān śrī-viṣṇu purāṇe “mat sambandhena vo gopā yadi lajjā na jāyate \| ślāghyo vāhaṁ tataḥ kiṁ vo vicāreṇa prayojanam \| yadi vo ‘smi mayi prītaḥ ślāghyo ‘haṁ bhavatām yadi \| tad ātma-bandhu-sadṛśī buddhir vaḥ kriyatāṁ mayi \|\| nāhaṁ devo na gandharvo na yakṣa no ca rākṣasaḥ \| ahaṁ vo bāndavo jāto nātaś cintyam ato ‘nyathā iti na cātra hṛt-kampa-hetoḥ sambhramasyodyāt mādhurya-bhāva-kṣatiḥ – “devo vā dānavo vā tvaṁ yakṣo gandharva eva vā \| kimvāsmākaṁ vicāreṇa bāndhavo ‘si namo ‘stu te \|\|” iti bandhu-bhāvasyaiva taiḥ sthāpanāt \| tasmat tad bhāvaṁ tan mukhāt śrotuṁ teṣāṁ tathāśaṅkothāpanam iti jñāyate \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The second type of devotee, attaining perfection by ruci-bhakti, enters the spiritual world and gain the association of Kṛṣṇa (in Vraja). This is the preferred goal since even Lakṣmī desires it.** ***nāyaṁ sukhāpo bhagavān*****, “Bhagavān Śrī Kṛṣṇa is not easily attainable.” (SB 10.21) These devotees are most dear to the Lord since there is deep taste in their affection for him. When the cowherds saw Kṛṣṇa lift Govardhana, they became astonished and conjecture that he was the supreme Lord. Kṛṣṇa then spoke.**

**ity evam uktas tair gopaiḥ kṛṣṇo 'py āha mahāmatiḥ**  
**mat-saṁbandhena vo gopā yadi lajjā na jāyate**  
**ślāghyo vāhaṁ tataḥ kiṁ vo vicāreṇa prayojanam**  
**yadi vo 'sti mayi prītiḥ ślāghyo 'haṁ bhavatāṁ yadi**  
**tad-ātma-bandhu-sadṛśī buddhir vaḥ kriyātāṁ mayi**

**When the cowherds spoke in this way, wise Kṛṣṇa was silent for a moment and then spoke with affectionate anger. O cowherds! If you are not ashamed of having a relationship with me, or if I am praiseworthy, then why think about this at all? If you have affection for me and if I am praised by you, you should think of me as the closest friend.** ***Viṣṇu Purāṇa*** **5.13.9-12**

**nāhaṁ devo na gandharvo na yakṣo na ca dānavaḥ \|**  
**ahaṁ vo bāndhavo jāto nātaś cintyam ato’nyathā \|\|**

**I am not a devatā, a Gandharva, a Yakṣa or Dānava. I am your friend. You should not think otherwise. (Viṣṇu Purāṇa 5.13.12)**

**In this case there is not harm done to the sentiments of sweetness which would be caused by manifestation of awe caused by fear. The cowherds establish their relationship as intimate friends:**

**devo vā dānavo vā tvaṁ yakṣo gandharva eva vā /**  
**kim asmākaṁ vicāreṇa bāndhavo 'si namostu te //**

**Are you a** ***devatā,*** **a demon, a Yakṣa, or a Gandharva? Thinking about this we conclude that you are our friend. We offer respects to you.**

**One should know that they raised this doubt in order to hear from Kṛṣṇa’s mouth the nature of their affectionate relationship. \|\|14\|\|**

### Text 15

*nanu kṛṣṇasya bhagavato yaśodaurasa-putratve tasmin vallavānāṁ gotratva-lakṣaṇo bandhu-bhāvaḥ syān na cāsau tad aurasas tathānukteḥ \| bhāva-bandhus tu bhavet “ye yathā mām” ityādi tad vākyāt \| “nandāmajaḥ gopikā-sutaḥ “ ityādi-vākyaṁ ṭu upaguhyātmajām ityādi-vad aupacārikātmajatva-paraṁ bhavīti cen, maivam etat \| “niśithe tama udbhūte jāyamane janārdane \| devakyā deva-rūpiṇyāṁ viṣṇuḥ sarva-guhāśayaḥ \| āvirāsīt yathā prācyāṁ diśīndur iva puṣkalaḥ” ity atra “ yaśodā nanda-patnī ca jātaṁ param abudhyate \| na tal liṅgaṁ pariśrāntā nidrayāpagat smṛtiḥ” ity atra ca śrī śukenaiva tad aurasatvaysokteḥ \| yaśodāpi devakīty ucyate “ deve nāmnī nanda-bhāryāyā yaśodā devakīti ca” ity adi-purāṇa-vacanāt \| deva-rūpiṇyām ity uktes tad- garbha-sambandho ‘pi na doṣāvahaḥ \| vasudeva-patnī-vat nanda-patnī ca paraṁ pareśam eva sva-garbhāj jātam abudhyata \| tasya vasudevāgamāder liṅgaṁ cihnaṁ nābudhyata yataḥ parīty adi \| “nanda-gopa-gṛhe jāto yaśodā-garbha-sambhavaḥ” ityādi purāṇe sphuṭam ukteś ca \| itthaṁ ca nandātamja-gopikā-sutādi-śabdhair mukhyārthair bhāvyam \| nanu sphuṭārtha-prayayo viruddhaḥ syāt, maivam \| yaśodā-sutena saha devakī-sutasyaikyāt tad ekasya tasya mathurādiṣu gamanād antarāntarā vraje kurukṣetre cāgateḥ sphuṭārthasyāpy abhaṅgāt \| tatha cobhayathāpi tad bandhuḥ saḥ \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“If Kṛṣṇa, the supreme Lord, were the actual son of Yaśodā, the cowherds could accept him as their relative as a similar cowherd. But he was not born to Yaśodā. That is not stated. Taking him as their relative cannot be accepted (if he were actually not Yaśodā’s son) since it is said that according to one’s surrender, the Lord responds (*****ye yathā māṁ prapadyante*****). Statements like “the son of Nanda” or “the cowherd woman’s son” are metaphorical only.”**

**That is not so. The following statements indicate that Kṛṣṇa was born to Yaśodā:**

***niśīthe tama-udbhūte jāyamāne janārdane***  
***devakyāṁ deva-rūpiṇyāṁ viṣṇuḥ sarva-guhā-śayaḥ***  
***āvirāsīd yathā prācyāṁ diśīndur iva puṣkalaḥ***

**Then the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Viṣṇu, who is situated in the core of everyone’s heart, appeared from the heart of Devakī (or Yaśodā) in the dense darkness of night, like the full moon rising on the eastern horizon, because Devakī was of the same category as Śrī Kṛṣṇa. SB 10.3.8**

***yaśodā nanda-patnī ca jātaṁ param abudhyata***  
***na tal-liṅgaṁ pariśrāntā nidrayāpagata-smṛtiḥ***

**Exhausted by the labor of childbirth, Yaśodā was overwhelmed with sleep and understood that the Lord (*****param*****) had been born to her but did not understand that Vasudeva had come (*****tal-liṅgam*****). SB 10.3.53**

**Devakī is another name of Yaśodā:**

***dve nāmnī nanda-bhāryāyā,***

**The wife of Nanda had two names, Yasodā and also Devakī.** ***Bṛhad-viṣṇu Purāṇa***

**It is not a fault if Kṛṣṇa is related also to Devakī’s womb, as indicated by the words** ***deva-rūpiṇyām*****. Like Devakī, Yaśodā understood that the supreme Lord (*****param*****) had been born in her womb but she did not know that Vasudeva had come (*****tal liṅgam*****), since she was exhausted and overcome with sleep. It is clearly said in the Purāṇas:**

**nanda-gopa-gṛhe jāto yaśodā-garbha-sambhavaḥ**  
**Kṛṣṇa took birth from Yaśodā’s womb in Nanda’s house. Ādi Purāṇa**

**Thus it should be accepted that the main meaning (not metaphorical) is that Kṛṣna is the son of Nanda and Yaśodā.**

**“That is contrary to the direct meaning of the words (*****devakī-putra*****).” That is not so, since Yaśodā’s son and Devakī’s son are one person. This is not contrary to the later descriptions where the same Kṛṣṇa goes to Mathurā, and then goes to Vraja and Kurukṣetra. He is the son of Yaśodā in both mood and actual birth relationship. \|\|15\|\|**

## Text 16,17,18

*tad evaṁ dvi-vidha-jñāna-bhakti-gocaraḥ kṛṣṇābhidhānaḥ puruṣottamaḥ krṭsna-śakti-vyañjī svayam bhagavān ucyate \| akṛtsna-tad-vyañjī dvy-eka-tad-vyañjī ca vilāso ‘ṁśaḥ kalā ceti “ete cāṁśa-kaḷaḥ puṁsaḥ kṛṣṇas tu bhagavān svayam “aṣṭaṁas tu tayor āsīt svayam eva hariḥ kila “ ityādi smṛteḥ \| nanu yasya puṁso matsyādayo ‘ṁśa-kalāḥ sah pumān eva kṛtsna-śakti-prakāśī krṣṇa ity artha-prayayān noktavyavastheti cet, maivaṁ : bhinnopakramārthakāt tu śabdāt brahma-saṁhitāyāṁ kṛṣṇa-rūpam prakṛtya trayāṇāṁ puruṣāṇāṁ ṛāmādīnāṁ ca tad avatāratvokteś ca\| tathāhi “īśvaraḥ paramaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ saccidānanda-vigrahaḥ anādir ādir govindaḥ sarva-kāraṇa-kāraṇam \| sahasra-patraṁ kamala lokulākhyaṁ mahat padam \| tat karṇikāraṁ tad dāma atad anantāṁśa-sambhavaṁ” ityādikam utvā “yaḥ kāraṇāranvale bhajati sma yoga-nidrām ananta-jagadaṇḍa-saroma-kūpaḥ \| ādhāra-śaktim avalambya paraṁ svamūrtiṁ govindam ādi-puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi \|\|” “yasyaika-niśvasita-kālam athāvalambya jīvanti loma-bilakjā jagadaṇdā-nāthāh viṣṇur mahān sa iha yasya kalā-viśeṣo govindam ādi” ityādi “dipārcer eva hi daśantaram abhyupetya dīpāyate vivṛta-hetu-samāna-dharmā \| yas tādṛg eva hi ca viṣṇu tayā vibhāti govindam” ityādi ca “rāmādimūrtiṣu kā-niyamena tiṣṭhan nānāvatāram akarod bhuvaneṣu kint \| kṛṣṇaḥ svayaṁ samabhavat paramaḥ pumān yo govindm ādi” ityaḍi ca \|\| 16,17,18\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The supreme Lord called Kṛṣṇa who is realized in two ways--through jñāna and bhakti, endowed with all śaktis, is called svayam Bhagavān. The forms which are not endowed with all the śaktis, manifesting one or two of the śaktis, are called aṁśa and kalā.**

**ete cāṁśa-kalāḥ puṁsaḥ kṛṣṇas tu bhagavān svayam**  
**These are aṁśas and kalās but Kṛṣṇa is svayam bhagavān.**  
**aṣṭamas tu tayor āsīt svayam eva hariḥ kila**

**But the eighth child of Vasudeva and Devakī is** ***svayam hari*****. SB 9.24.55**

**“You say the meaning is the form of whom others are aṁśas and kalā is full of all śaktis. But that is not what is stated.”**

**That is not so. The word** ***tu*** **indicates a different category. In Brahma-saṁhitā it is stated that the three puruṣāvatāras as well as Rāma and others are Kṛṣṇa’s avatāras.**

***īśvaraḥ paramaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ sac-cid-ānanda-vigrahaḥ***  
***anādir ādir govindaḥ sarva-kāraṇa-kāraṇam \|\|***

**Kṛṣṇa is the supreme controller. He is the enjoyer of innumerable consorts, and the possessor of an eternal form of knowledge and bliss. Though, he is without origin, he is the source of all other forms of God, and is the cause of all causes. He is Govinda, the boy who cares for the cows.**

***sahasra-patra-kamalaṁ gokulākhyaṁ mahat padam \|***  
***tat-karṇikāraṁ tad-dhāma tad-anantāṁśa-sambhavam \|\|***

**The** ***dhāma*** **of Kṛṣṇa called Gokula is the supreme abode. It is the center (pericarp) of a lotus with a thousand petals and it is sustained by a portion of the spiritual energy of Baladeva called Ananta.**

***yaḥ kāraṇārṇava-jale bhajati sma yoga-***  
***nidrām ananta-jagad-aṇḍa-sa-roma-kūpaḥ \|***  
***ādhāra-śaktim avalambya parāṁ sva-mūrtiṁ***  
***govindam ādi-puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi \|\|***

**I worship the Supreme Lord Govinda who, as Mahā-viṣṇu, enjoys** ***yoga-nidrā*** **in the waters of the Kāraṇa ocean while holding unlimited universes in the pores of his skin and lying upon the supporting energy of Śeṣa, another spiritual form of himself.**

***yasyaika-niśvasita-kālam athāvalambya***  
***jīvanti loma-vilajā jagad-aṇḍa-nāthāḥ \|***  
***viṣṇur mahān sa iha yasya kalā-viśeṣo***  
***govindam ādi-puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi \|\|\|***

**I worship the Supreme Lord Govinda whose first expansion is Mahā-viṣṇu, who determines the appearance and disappearance of the rulers within the universe (even Kṣirodakaśāyī-viṣṇu) by his exhalation and inhalation of breath.**

***dīpārcir eva hi daśāntaram abhyupetya***  
***dīpāyate vivṛta-hetu-samāna-dharmā \|***  
***yas tādṛg eva hi ca viṣṇutayā vibhāti***  
***govindam ādi-puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi \|\|***

**I worship the Supreme Lord Govinda who expands as Kṣīrodakaśāyī-viṣṇu (through Mahāviṣṇu and Garbodakaśāyī-viṣṇu), who is non-different from him, just as a large lamp lights up a second lamp, which lights up a third lamp -- which illuminates with the same quality of light as the original lamp.**

***rāmādi-mūrtiṣu kalā-niyamena tiṣṭhan***  
***nānāvatāram akarod bhuvaneṣu kintu \|***  
***kṛṣṇaḥ svayaṁ samabhavat paramaḥ pumān yo***  
***govindam ādi-puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi \|\|***

**I worship the Supreme Lord Govinda who, though appearing in the worlds in eternal forms such as Rāma and Nṛsiṁha with their characteristic powers, also appears personally in the supreme form of Kṛṣṇa with the most attractive pastimes. \|\|16,17,18\|\|**

### Text 19

**Перевод:**

**yat tu “pūrṇam adaḥ pūrṇam idaṁ pūrṇāt pūrṇam udacyate \| pūrṇasya pūrṇam ādāya pūrṇam evāvaśiṣyate” iti śrutyā “sarve nityāḥ śāśvatāś ca devās tasya parātmanaḥ \| hānopādāna-rahitā naiva prakṛti-jāḥ kvacit \| paramānanda-sandhohā jñāna-mātrāś ca sarvataḥ \| sarve sva-guṇaiḥ pūṛṇāḥa sarva-doṣa-vivarjitāḥ” iti mahā-vārāhoktyā ca \| sarveṣāṁ sva-rūpaṇāṁ pūrṇatva-pratyayāt katamad ananyāpekṣi svayaṁ rūpaṁ iti na śakyaṁ niścetum iti kāścid āha \| tad apeśalam – svarūpa-bhede satyāsyāḥ śaṅkāyāḥ pravṛtteḥ \| na catra tad bhedaḥ \| eka eva kṛṣṇaḥ pareśo vaidūyravat ātmani nāna-rūpāṇi vyañjī--“eko vaśī sarvagaḥ kṛṣṇa īḍya eko pi san bahudā yo vibhāti” ityād śruteḥ \| yathaikasyaiva ṣaṭ śāstrāṅgasya viduṣaḥ kvacit kṛtsnaśāstrodgrāhiṇaḥ sarva-jñātvaṁ pratīyate kvacid kṛtsna-tadgrāhiṇaḥ dvyekatad prāhiṇaś ca sarva-jña-kalpatavṁ cetyevam ekasya bahv-alpa-śakti-vyakti-savyapekṣiṇo ‘ṁśitvāṁśatvādīni \| evaṁ evoktaṁ vṛddhaiḥ—“ekatvaṁ ca prthaktvaṁ ca yathāṁśatvaṁ utāṁśitā \| āsmin nekatra nāyuktaṁ acintyānanta-śaktitaḥ \|\| śakter vyaktis tathāvyakits tāratamyasya kāraṇam” iti \| dvecchayā nānā-śakti-prakāśitvam aśmitvaṁ tasyaiva sarvadālpa-śakti-prakāśtivaṁ aṁśatvam \|\|19\|\|**

**Śruti says:**

***oṁ pūrṇam adaḥ pūrṇam idaṁ***  
***pūrṇāt pūrṇam udacyate***  
***pūrṇasya pūrṇam ādāya***  
***pūrṇam evāvaśiṣyate***

**The unmanifest** ***brahman*** **is perfect. The manifested** ***brahman*** **is perfect. From the unmanifest** ***brahman*** **appears the manifest** ***brahman*** **as various** ***avatāras.*** **When the manifested forms emerge from the unmanifest** ***brahman*** **the unmanifest** ***brahman*** **remains. Īśopaniṣad**

**Mahā-varāha Purāṇa says:**

***sarve nityāḥ śāśvatāś ca dehās tasya parātmanaḥ \|***  
***hānopādāna-rahitā naiva prakṛtijāḥ kvacit \|\|***  
***paramānanda-sandohā jñāna-mātrāś ca sarvataḥ \|***  
***sarve sarva-guṇaiḥ pūrṇā sarva-doṣa-vivarjitāḥ*** **\|\|**

**All the forms of the Lord are eternal, appearing constantly within the material world with bodies of Paramātmā, without any destructible elements made of** ***prakṛti*****. They are completely filled with the highest bliss and knowledge, full of all good qualities and devoid of all faults.**

**“Since it is established that all the forms of the Lord are complete, one cannot say that one is the svayam rūpa upon which all depend.”**

**This is not proper. One should not speculate that there are different forms. There is no difference in the Lord. One Kṛṣṇa, the supreme Lord, like a vaidūyra gem, manifests many forms in himself.**

**Kṛṣṇa is supreme, all-pervading, and most worthy of praise. Though he is one, he is also many. Gopāla-tāpanī Upaniṣad**

**A learned person who knows the six aṅgas of scripture sometimes uses all scriptures in arguments and appears to be all knowing. Sometimes he only uses one or two scriptures but is still accepted as all knowing. Similarly the Lord sometimes manifests many śaktis and sometimes a few, and thus appears as the aṁaśī (source) or the aṁśa.**

***ekatvaṁ ca pṛthaktvaṁ ca tathāṁśatvam utāṁśitā \|***  
***tasminn ekatra nāyuktam acintyānanta-śaktitaḥ*** **\|\|**

**It is not unsuitable for Kṛṣṇa to be one and many,** ***aṁśī*** **and** ***aṁśa*** **because he has unlimited inconceivable energy. Laghu-bhāgavatāmṛta**

***œakter vyaktis tathâvyaktis târatamyasya kâra?am***

**The manifestation and non-manifestation of** ***œakti*****is the cause of grading the** ***a?úî*** **and the** ***a?œa*****. Laghu-bhāgavatāmṛta**

**By his will he manifests various śaktis and appears as the aṁśi or, manifesting a few śaktis, he appears as the aṁśa. \|\|19\|\|**

### Text 20

*etena parama-vyomādhipādi-rūpo ‘pi san kṛṣṇah sarvān guṇān prakāśayet, tatrapi guṇa-pūrṇatvād iti nirastam \| yat tv atharva-śirasi puruṣāvatārāt “atha puruṣo via nārāyaṇo ‘kāmayat” ityādinā viśvā-sṛṣtim uktvā “brahmaṇo devakī putraḥ” iti tasyaiva kṛṣṇatvam uktvā kaścit babhrāma tac ca praty uktaṁ tat-kesatvaṁ tu sudūrāpās tam ukta-nirṇayāt “nārāyaṇo ‘ṅgaṁ nara-bhū-jalānayāt” iti smṛteś ca \| kintu kṛṣṇasyaiva puruṣāvatāritvaṁ tatroktam iti suṣṭhu --anyathoktārtha-vaiparītyāpatteḥ \|\|20\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By this, the argument that Kṛṣṇa, since he is the form ruling the spiritual sky, should manifest all qualities is defeated. The Atharva-śīras, having described the creation of the universe, saying “The puruṣa, Nārāyaṇa desired to create” then describes Kṛṣṇa as the son of Devakī, identifying Nārāyaṇa with Kṛṣṇa. Some draw erroneous conclusions but these have been answered. Claims that Kṛṣṇa is the hair of Viṣṇu is completely refuted.**

**nārāyaṇo 'ṅgaṁ nara-bhū-jalāyanāt**

**Nārāyaṇa is simply your expansion. He is the cause of the primeval waters. SB 10.14.14**

**This indicates that Kṛṣṇa is the source of the puruṣāvatāras since the opposite meaning would be contrary to the context**

### Text 21

*yac ca tasmin eva “yo vai śrī-rāmacandraḥ sa bhagavān, ye matsya-kūrmādayo ‘vatārās tasmai vai namo namaḥ” iti sitāpater matsyādy-avataritvaṁ uktaṁ tat kila yuktam eva, tasya paravyomādhipati-rūpatvāt \| yac ca “trayāṇām api lokānāṁ adi kartā svayaṁ prabhuḥ” iti śrī-rāmāyaṇe, “adhika-sāmya-vimukta-dhāmnaḥ” iti bhāgavate ca tasya svayaṁ pada-bodhyatvam uktaṁ tad api matsyādy-āmśitvābhiprāyenaiva neyam \| kṛṣnād aviśeṣe svīkṛte tu “rāmadimūrtiṣu kalā niyamena tiṣṭhan ity uktiḥ dṛṣṭarāṁāṇāṁ api danḍakāraṇya-nivāsināṁ maharṣīṇāṁ kṛṣṇa-spṛhoktiś ca vyākupyeta \| pūrṇām idaḥ ityādīni vākyāni svarūpa satsarva-guṇāni sarvāṇi ityāhur na t abhivyakta-sarval-guṇ;anīti \| tasmāt sarvaādbhivyakasarva-śaktitvāt kṛṣṇasyaiva svayaṁ rūpatvaṁ siddham \|\|21\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**It is proper to say that Matsya and others are avatāras of Rāma as in the following, since Rāma is the lord of Vaikuṇṭha:**

**yo vai śrī-rāmacandraḥ sa bhagavān, ye matsya-kūrmādayo ‘vatārās tasmai vai namo namaḥ**

**Rāmacandra is bhagavān. I offer respects to his avatāras such as Matsya and Kūrma. Rāma-tāpanī Upaniṣad**

**Rāmāyaṇa says:**

**trayāṇām api lokānāṁ adi kartā svayaṁ prabhuḥ**

**Rāma is the master of the three worlds. (Rāmāyaṇa)**

**Bhāgavatam says:**

**adhika-sāmya-vimukta-dhāmnaḥ**

**No one is greater than or equal to Rāma. (SB 9.11.20)**

**The word** ***svayam*** **applied to Rāma indicates that he is the aṁśī of Matsya and other forms. But to claim that Rāmacandra and Kṛṣṇa are then the same is contradicted by Brahma-saṁhitā:**

***rāmādi-mūrtiṣu kalā-niyamena tiṣṭhan***  
***nānāvatāram akarod bhuvaneṣu kintu \|***  
***kṛṣṇaḥ svayaṁ samabhavat paramaḥ pumān yo***  
***govindam ādi-puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi \|\|***

**I worship the Supreme Lord Govinda who, though appearing in the worlds in eternal forms such as Rāma and Nṛsiṁha with their characteristic powers, also appears personally in the supreme form of Kṛṣṇa with the most attractive pastimes.**

**This would also contradict the fact that the great sages living in Daṇdakāraṇya, having seen Rāma, desired to attain Kṛṣṇa. Statements such as** ***pūrṇam adaḥ*** **indicate that Kṛṣṇa has all qualities in his svarūpa, but that all qualities are not manifested in other forms. Kṛṣna is the svayam rūpa because all śaktis are always manifested in him. \|\|21\|\|**

### Text 22

*śrī-kṛṣṇe hi rādhādyāḥ pūrṇāḥ śaktayaḥ sarvātiśayi-prema-pūrṇa-pārṣadatva-brahmādi-tattva-jña-vismāpaka-sthira-cara-vimohaka-veṇu-nāda-mādhurya-sva-paryānta-sarva-vismāpaka-rūpa-mādhurya-niratiṣaya-kāruṇyādayo guṇāḥ śrī-śukadibhir anubhūtāḥ sarvadāvirbhavanti, na tv anyatra \| puruṣa-bodhinyām atharvopaniṣadi śruyate – “gokulākhye māthura-maṇḍale vṛndāvana-madhye sahasra-dala-padma-madhye kalpa-taror mūle aṣṭa-dala-keśare govindo ‘pi śyāmaḥ pitāmbaro dvibhujo mayura-puccha-śiro veṇu-vetra-hasto nirguṇaḥ saguṇo nirākāraḥ sākāro nirīhaḥ saceṣṭo vivājate \| dve pārśve candrāvalī rādhikā ca” iti “yasyā aṁśe lakṣmī-durgādikā śaktiḥ“ iti, agre ca “tasyādyā prakṛtī rādhikā nitya-nirguṇa-sarvālaṅkara-śobhitā prasannāśeṣa-lāvaṇya-sundarī” ityādi, Ṛk-pariśiṣṭe ca “rādhayā mādhavo devo mādhvenaiva rādhikā vibhrājante janeṣu “ iti \|\|22\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The complete śaktis such as Rādhā and others, the qualities such as mercy,the sweetness of his form which astonish all beings and even himself, the sweetness of his flute which bewilders all plants and animals and astonishes knowers of truth like Brahmā and even his close associates filled with intense prema--qualities realized by great souls like Śukadeva--are present in Kṛṣna and no one else. In the Puruṣa-bodhi Upaniṣad it is said:**

**gokulākhye māthura-maṇḍale vṛndāvana-madhye sahasra-dala-padma-madhye kalpa-taror mūle aṣṭa-dala-keśare govindo ‘pi śyāmaḥ pitāmbaro dvibhujo mayura-puccha-śiro veṇu-vetra-hasto nirguṇaḥ saguṇo nirākāraḥ sākāro nirīhaḥ saceṣṭo vivājate \| dve pārśve candrāvalī rādhikā ca**

**In the place called Gokula, within Vṛndāvana within Mathurā district., within a thousand petelled lotus, at the base of a desire tree amidst the filaments of the eight petalled lotus resides Govinda, dark in complexion, wearing yellow cloth and peacock feather on his head, possessing two arms, holding a flute and stick in his hands, who is beyond the material guṇas and possessed of all qualities, who has no material form and has a spiritual form, who has no material activity and all spiritual activities. On his two sides are Candrāvalī and Rādhā.**

**yasyā aṁśe lakṣmī-durgādikā śaktiḥ**

**Lakṣmī and Durgā are portions of her śakti. Puruṣa-bodhi Upaniṣad**

**tasyādyā prakṛtī rādhikā nitya-nirguṇa-sarvālaṅkara-śobhitā prasannāśeṣa-lāvaṇya-sundarī**

**Rādhā is the original energy, endowed with all ornaments and eternally beyond the material guṇas, pleasing with unlimited beauty and grace. Rādhā Upaniṣad**

**Ṛk-pariśiṣṭa says:**

**rādhayā mādhavo devo mādhvenaiva rādhikā vibhrājante janeṣu**

**Rādhā shines with Kṛṣṇa and Kṛṣṇa shines with Rādhā among the people. \|\|22\|\|**

### Text 23

*gautamīye tantre caivaṁ smaryate –“sattvaṁ tattvaṁ paratvaṁ ca tattva-trayaṁ ahaṁ kila \| tri-tattva-rūpiṇī sāpi rādhikā mama vallabhā \|\| prakṛteḥ para evāhaṁ sāpi ca chakti-rūpiṇī \| devī kṛṣṇa-mayī proktā rādhikā para-devatā sarva-lakṣmīmayī sarva-kāntiḥ sammnohinī parā “ iti \| iha kṛṣṇa-māyī paretyanenedam uktam “parāsya ṣaktir vivvdhāiva śruyate svābhāvikī jñāna-bala-kriyā ca” iti śrutyā, “yātīti-gocarā vācāṁ manasāṁ cāviśeṣaṇā \| jñāna-vijñāna-paricchedyā vande tām īśvarīṁ parāṁ” iti smrtyā ca \| yā bhavad abhinnābhihitā yā ca hlādiṇīytyādinā viśeṣitā, sā paraiva rādhikeśvarīti , tathā ca hlādinī-saṁvit-sārṁaśa-premātnikā seti kāmādyadhikaryna-bhāṣhe sphuṭaṁ etat \|\|23\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In Gautamīya-tantra it is said:**

***sattvaṁ tattvaṁ paratvaṁ ca tattva-trayam ahaṁ kila \|***  
***tri-tattva-rūpiṇī sāpi rādhikā mama vallabhā \|\|***  
***prakṛteḥ para evāhaṁ sāpi mac-chakti-rūpiṇī \|***

**I am the three principles: sattva (effect), tattva (case) and paratva (spiritual energy). The form of these three is Rādhikā my consort.I am beyond prakṛti and she is my śakti.**

***devī kṛṣṇa-mayī proktā rādhikā para-devatā \|***  
***sarva-lakṣmī-mayī sarva-kāntiḥ saṁmohinī parā \|\|***

**Rādhā, non-different from Kṛṣṇa, is the supreme devatā. All Lakṣmīs reside within her. She has all beauty, bewilders everyone and is supreme.**

***Kṛṣṇa-mayī para-devatā*** **indicates that Rādhā is the supreme śakti. Śruti says:**

***parāsya śaktir vividhaiva śrūyate***  
***svābhāvikī jñāna-bala-kriyā ca \|\|***

**His supreme power is manifold. His actions are naturally revealed with knowledge and strength. (Śvetāśvatara Upaniṣad)**

**Smṛti says:**

**yātīta-gocarā vācāṁ manasāṁ cāviśeṣaṇā \|**  
**jñāni-jñāna-paricchedyā vande tām īśvarīṁ parām \|\|**

**I offer respects to your spiritual energy which is ascertained by knowledge of the jñānīs, which is beyond mind and words and is without material distinctions. (Viṣṇu Purāṇa 1.19.77)**

**The śakti described as nondifferent from the Lord, and which is called hlādinī, is Rādhikā, the supreme ruler, who is composed of the prema which is the essence of the hlādinī and saṁvit śaktis. This is made clear in the commentary on the** ***kāmādy-adhikāraṇa*** **of Brahma-sūtra. \|\|23\|\|**

*evaṁ sati tat tu skāndhe kācit narttakī śrī-mūrter mādhurīṁ dṛṣṭvā tad agre prabodhanī-jāgaran nṛtyād ṛādhābhud ity ukam tatra kila “brahmaiva san brahmāpyeti ityādi vat tat sādṛśyaṁ agamad ity eva mantavyam \|\|24\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In the Skanda Purāṇa it is described that a female dancer danced all night in front of the deity on seeing the sweetness of that form. She then became Rādhā. One should understand that this means that she attained similarity to Rādhā, just as by meditating on Brahman one becomes like Brahman. \|\|24\|\|**

### Text 25

*tad evaṁ mahā-lakṣmītvād eva śrī-rādhāyāḥ pūrṇatvaṁ nirvādham \| śrī-kṛṣṇa-preyasyaḥ khalu sarvā-lakṣmya eva tan mukhyatvāt, sā tu mahā-lakṣmīr iti suśliṣṭaṁ “cintāmaṇi-prakara-sadmasu kalpa-vṛkṣa-lakṣāvṛteṣu surabhīr abhipālayantam \| lakṣmī-sahasra-śata-sambhrama-sevyamānaṁ govindam ādi-puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi \|****”*** *ityādeḥ \|****“****śriyaḥ kāntāḥ kāntaḥ parama-puruṣaḥ” ityādeś ca “gopyo labdhvācyutaṁ kāntaṁ śriya ekānta-vallabham” ityādeś ca “yathā rādhā priyā viṣnos yastāḥ kuṇḍaṁ piryaṁ tathā \| sarvagopīṣu saivaikā viṣnor atyanta-vallabhā” iti pādmāt, “trailokye pṛthivī dhanyā tatra vṛndāvanaṁ purī \| tatrāpi gopīkāḥ pārtha tatra rādhābhidhā mama” ityādi purāṇāc ca tasyā aṁśe lakṣmī-dugrādikā śaktir iti śrutis tu darśitāiva \|\|25\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Thus there is no dispute that Rādhā is complete, since she is Mahālakṣmī. All Kṛṣṇa’s lovers are Lakṣmīs but she is the chief.**

***cintāmaṇi-prakara-sadmasu kalpa-vṛkṣa-***  
***lakṣāvṛteṣu surabhīr abhipālayantam \|***  
***lakṣmī-sahasra-śata-sambhrama-sevyamānaṁ***  
***govindam ādi-puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi \|\|***

**I worship the Supreme Lord Govinda who affectionately tends the cows in stables whose walls are made of** ***cintāmaṇi*****, surrounded by desire trees. He is eagerly served by countless** ***gopīs.***

***śriyaḥ kāntāḥ kāntaḥ parama-puruṣaḥ***

**There are unlimited** ***gopīs*** **and their beloved Kṛṣṇa, the supreme form of God. Brahma-saṁhitā**

**gopyo labdhvācyutaṁ kāntaṁ śriya ekānta-vallabham**

**Having attained as their intimate lover Acyuta, the exclusive consort of the goddess of fortune, the gopīs enjoyed great pleasure. SB 10.33.14**

***yathā rādhā priyā viṣṇos tasyāḥ kuṇḍaṁ priyaṁ tathā \|***  
***sarva-gopīṣu saivaikā viṣṇor atyanta-vallabhā \|\|***

**Just as Rādhā is dear to Kṛṣṇa, so is her kuṇḍa. Among all the gopīs she is dearest to Kṛṣṇa. (Padma Purāṇa)**

***trailokye pṛthivī dhanyā tatra vṛndāvanaṁ punaḥ \|***  
***tatrāpi gopikāḥ pārtha tatra rādhābhidhā mama \|\|***

**In the three worlds earth is most fortunate. On earth Vṛndāvana is most auspicious. In Vṛndāvana, my gopī named Rādhā is most fortunate. (Ādi Purāṇa)**

**It was shown in the śrutis (Puruṣa-bodhinī śruti) that Lakṣmī, Durgā and others are Rādhā’s aṁśas. \|\|25\|\|**

### Text 26

*śrī-kṛṣṇa-parikarāṇām asamābhyadhika-prematvaṁ tad-artha-jvalad-viṣa-hrāda-preveśadhāvanāditaḥ pratīyate yastmāt bhagavto pi gāḍa-vaśyateti niveditaṁ brahmaṇā “eṣāṁ ghoṣa-nivāsinām uta bhavān kiṁ deva rāteti naś \| ceto viśva-phalāt phalaṁ tvad-aparaṁ kutrāpy ayan muhyati \| sad-veṣād iva pūtanāpi sa-kulā tvām eva devāpitā \| yad-dhāmārtha-suhṛt-priyātma-tanaya-prāṇāśayās tvat-kṛte \|\|” \|\|26\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa’s associates have unequalled prema. In order to save Kṛṣṇa, they ran forward to enter the lake full of intense poison. Kṛṣṇa is controlled by that prema. Brahmā said:**

**eṣāṁ ghoṣa-nivāsinām uta bhavān kiṁ deva rāteti naś**  
**ceto viśva-phalāt phalaṁ tvad-aparaṁ kutrāpy ayan muhyati**  
**sad-veṣād iva pūtanāpi sa-kulā tvām eva devāpitā**  
**yad-dhāmārtha-suhṛt-priyātma-tanaya-prāṇāśayās tvat-kṛte**

**O Lord! What can you give to the people of Vraja whose houses, possessions, friends, dear ones, bodies, sons, life airs and hearts are dedicated only to you, since there is nothing superior to you or Vraja (which you have already given)? Thinking of this, my mind becomes bewildered. You awarded yourself even to Pūtanā and her family members because she wore the disguise of a nurse. SB 10.14.35 \|\|26\|\|**

### Text 27

*druhiṇādi-tattvavid-viṣmapaka-sthiracara-vihohaka veṇu-nāda-madhuryam tu “savanaśas tad upadhārya sureśāḥ śakra-śarva-parameṣṭhi-purogāḥ \| kavaya ānata-kandhara-cittāḥ kaśmalaṁ yayur aniścita-tattvāḥ \|\|” “kā stry aṅga te kala-padāyata-veṇu-gīta- sammohitārya-caritān na calet tri-lokyām \| trailokya-saubhagam idaṁ ca nirīkṣya rūpaṁ \| yad go-dvija-druma-mṛgāḥ pulakāny abibhran” ity ādibhyaḥ sthira-cara-vimohaka-rūpa-mādhuryaṁ coktam \|\|27\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The sweetness of his flute astonishes ŚIva and other knowers of truth, and bewilders all moving and nonmoving beings:**

***savanaśas tad upadhārya sureśāḥ śakra-śarva-parameṣṭhi-purogāḥ***  
***kavaya ānata-kandhara-cittāḥ kaśmalaṁ yayur aniścita-tattvāḥ***

**Brahmā, Śiva, Indra and other chief demigods become confused upon hearing the sound. Although they are the most learned authorities, they cannot ascertain the essence of that music, and thus they bow down their heads and hearts. SB 10.35.15**

**The moving and nonmoving beings are also bewildered by the sweetness of his beauty:**

***kā stry aṅga te kala-padāyata-veṇu-gīta-***  
***sammohitārya-caritān na calet tri-lokyām***  
***trailokya-saubhagam idaṁ ca nirīkṣya rūpaṁ***  
***yad go-dvija-druma-mṛgāḥ pulakāny abibhran***

**Dear Kṛṣṇa, what woman in all the three worlds wouldn’t deviate from religious behavior when bewildered by the sweet, drawn-out melody of your flute? Your beauty makes all three worlds auspicious. Indeed, even the cows, birds, trees and deer manifest the ecstatic symptom of bodily hair standing on end when they see your beautiful form. SB 10.29.40 \|\|27\|\|**

### Text 28

*“yan martya-līlaupayikaṁ sva-yoga-māyā-balaṁ darśayatā gṛhītam \| vismāpanaṁ svasya ca saubhagarddheḥ paraṁ padaṁ bhūṣaṇa-bhūṣaṇāṅgam” ityādibhyas tu sva-paryanta-sarva-vismpāka-rūpa-mādhuryam \|\|28\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The sweetness of his form astonishes even himself. That is described in verses like the following:**

**yan martya-līlaupayikaṁ sva-yoga-**  
**māyā-balaṁ darśayatā gṛhītam**  
**vismāpanaṁ svasya ca saubhagarddheḥ**  
**paraṁ padaṁ bhūṣaṇa-bhūṣaṇāṅgam**

**Kṛṣṇa possesses that form suitable for human pastimes to show the full capacity of his** ***yoga-māyā.*** **That form astonishes even the lord of Vaikuṇṭha. It is the pinnacle of auspicious qualities and enhances the beauty of his ornaments. SB 3.2.12 \|\|28\|\|**

### Text 29

**Перевод:**

**“aho bakī yaṁ stana-kāla-kūṭaṁ jighāṁsayāpāyayad apy asādhvī \| lebhe gatiṁ dhātry-ucitāṁ tato ’nyaṁ kaṁ vā dayāluṁ śaraṇaṁ vrajema \|\|” ityādhibyās tu niratiśaya-kāruṇyām \|\|29\|\|**

**His extreme mercy is illustrated in the following:**

**aho bakī yaṁ stana-kāla-kūṭaṁ**  
**jighāṁsayāpāyayad apy asādhvī**  
**lebhe gatiṁ dhātry-ucitāṁ tato ’nyaṁ**  
**kaṁ vā dayāluṁ śaraṇaṁ vrajema**

**Oh! Evil Pūtanā, who offered her poisonous breast to Kṛṣṇa to drink with the intention of killing him, attained the position of a nurse in the spiritual world. How else is so merciful? I surrender to him! SB 3.2.23 \|\|29\|\|**

### Text 30

*premādīnāṁ hlādinī-saṁvit-sārāṇām ānanda-cid-ātmaka-brahma-svarūpa-cintana-sa-manuṣayitvān mukyatvaṁ bodhyam \| tasmāt sarva-dharmāviṣkārāt kṛṇasya svayaṁ rūpatvaṁ tasyaivānāviṣkṛta-sarva-dharmasya paravyomādhipatvam \| tat tad-dharmāviṣkāra-tāratamyād eva kṛṣne ‘pi pūrṇātamatvādy-avasthāḥ kīrtitāḥ \|\|30\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Prema and higher experiences like pranaya etc.**

### Text 31

*sa eṣa bhagavān svarūpa-śakti-siddhe vicitre dhamni sva-sadṛśaiḥ pārṣadair viśiṣṭo nityaṁ līlāyate “sa bhagavaḥ kasmin pratiṣṭhitaḥ” iti “sve mahimni” iti “divye pure hy eṣa saṁvyomny ātmā pratiṣṭhitaḥ” iti, “tām vāṁ vāstuny usmasi gamadhye yatra gāvo bhuri-śṛṅgā ayāsaḥ” atrābhuḥ “tad urugāyasa vṛṣṇaḥ paramaṁ padam avabhāti bhūri “ iti ca śravanāt \| na khalu sva-mahima-pura-pratiṣṭhā sva-sadṛśān pārṣadān vinā sambhavati \| na ca tāsāṁ śubhāvaha-vidhi-rūpāṇāṁ gavāṁ ca līlayā vinā syad upayogaḥ \| “tām” ity adau – tām tāni vāṁ yuvayo rādhikā-kṛṣṇayor vāstūni dhāmāni gamadhye labdhum uṣmasi kāmayāmahe bhūri-śṛṅgāḥ praśasta-viṣāṇāḥ vṛṣṇaḥ svajanābhilāsa-varṣiṇa iti vyākhyeyam \| rādhāyety ādau laṭ prayogeṇa taiḥ saha tayor nitya-sāhityāvedanāl līlā nityeti sūcitaṁ \| “yasyā aṁśe lakṣmī-durgādikā śakti” ity etad vākyānantaraṁ paścimādi-krameṇa sarveṣāṁ paścime sammukhe lalitetyadibhir nirdiṣṭanāmnāṁ parikarāṇām kṛṣṇam abhitaḥ sthitir darśitād eva ceti pratijñatam viṣphuṭam \|\|31\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord is eternally endowed with associates of a similar nature in a variegated abode which arises from his svarūpa-śakti.**

***sa bhagavaḥ kasmin pratiṣṭhita iti sve mahimni***

**Dear sir, in what is the supreme Lord situated? He is situated in his own glorious abode. (Chāndogya Upaniṣad 7.24.1)**

***tāṁ vāṁ vāstūny uśmasi gamadhyai***  
***yatra gāvo bhūri-śṛṅgā ayāsaḥ \|***  
***atrāha tad urugāyasya vṛṣṇaḥ***  
***paramaṁ padam avabhāti bhūri \|\|***

**We desire to go (*****gamadhyai)*** **to the (*****tām*****) pastime places (*****vāstūni)*** **of Rādhā and Kṛṣna (*****vām*****) where cows with large (*****bhūri*****) auspicious (*****ayāsaḥ)*** **horns dwell. This supreme place of Svayam Bhagavān** ***(urugāyasya)*** **whose feet fulfill all desires (*****vṛṣṇaḥ*****) shines brilliantly** ***(avabhāti*****).**

**(Ṛg Veda 1.154.6)**

**The Lord, situated in his great abode, is not without his associates of similar nature. And he is not without auspicious cows for pastimes.** ***Tām*** **stands for** ***tāni. vām*** **indicates Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.** ***uśmasi gamadhyai***  **means “we desire to attain.”** ***Bhūr-śṛṅgāḥ*** **means endowed with excellent horns.** ***Vṛṣṇa*** **means “showering desirables on his devotees.” The present tense is used to indicate the eternal relationship of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa in their pastimes. In Puruṣa-bodhinī-śruti it is said:**

**paścime sammukhe lalitā, vāyavye śyāmalā, uttare śrīmati, aiśanyāṁ haripriyā, pūrve viśākhā, cāgnau śraddhā, yāmyāṁ padmā nairṛtyāṁ bhadrā.**

**To the west, facing him is Lalitā. To the northwest is Śyāmalā, to the north is Rādhā, to the north east is Haripriyā, to the east is Viśākhā, to the south east is Śraddhā, and to the south is Padmā and to the south wast is Bhadrā.**

**Thus it is shown that Kṛṣṇa is surrounded by his associates such as Lalitā. What was promised has been clearly shown to be true according to scripture. \|\|31\|\|**

### Text 32

*idam atra tattvam \| ananta-vijñānānanda-vapuṣo bhagavataḥ sva-cic-cakti-vilāsa-mayaṁ prakṛti-sparśa-śūnyam aparicchinnaṁ sva-mahima-saṁvyoma-śabditaṁ puraṁ ativistīrṇa-bahu-bhuma-prasādo ‘paraṁ cakāsti \| yatra nānāvirbhāva-parikara-paricchada-sva-cchandasamāveśocitāny ativiśālanī-bṛn-nicayamayāni nirupama-śakra-nīla-kuruvinda-candrakāntadikāntani vicitra prācīra-catvara-prasādādi-mahāvāsāni maṇi-citra-taṭika-piyūṣa-pūrṇa-sarit-sarovāpīkūpāni karpūra-pūryamāna-rajāṁsi pratikśaṇa-samullasat taru-vallī-gulmāni ramya-vihaṅgādi-sattva-sandohāni kamanīya-vimāna-valivyanti dhāmāni sphuranti \| yeṣu paramālaukika-rūpa-guṇa-saṁpannā muktā nitya-muktāś ca sarvābhyahitayā śrīdevyā saha vividha-vinodavantaṁ bhagavatṁ nānā-vidhair upacārair anukūlayantīti \|\|32\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**This is the meaning. There are huge, spiritual palaces, in a city called Saṁvyoma, which has no limits and is untouched by matter, and which is filled with the cit-śakti of the Lord whose body has unlimted knowledge and bliss. There are huge buildings, yards and walls of various colors, made of uneqalled sapphires, rubies and moonstones, containing various furniture for the comfortable living of the Lord’s associates. There are wells, lakes and rivers filled with sweet water and decorated with banks covered with gems. There is sand made of camphor and there are shrubs, creepers and trees shining at every moment. There are groups of attractive birds and animals. There are attractive places traversed by beautiful flying vehicles. In these places, liberated souls and eternally liberated souls endowed with most astonishing forms and qualities, favorably serve the Lord along with his consort, using various objects of worship. \|\|32\|\|**

### Text 33

*evam evoktaṁ jitanta-stotre-“lokaṁ vaikuṇṭhanāmānaṁ divyaṣāḍ guṇya-saṁyutam \| avaiṣṇāvānām aprāpyaṁ guṇa-traya-vivarjitam \|\| nitya-siddhaiḥ samākīrṇāṁ tan-mayaiḥ pāñca-kālikaiḥ \|sabhā-prāsāda-saṁyuktaṁ vanaiś copavanaiḥ śubham \|\| vāpī-kūpa-taḍāgaiś ca vṛkṣa-ṣaṇḍaiḥ sumaṇḍitam \| aprākṛtaṁ surair vandyam ayutārka-sama-prabham \|\| prakṛṣṭa-sattva-śaktiṁ tvāṁ kadā drakṣyāmi cakṣuṣā \| krīḍantaṁ ramayā sārddhaṁ līlābhūmiṣu keśavam” ityādi \|\|33\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**In the Jitanta-stotra it is said:**

***lokaṁ vaikuṇṭha-nāmānaṁ divya-ṣaḍ-guṇa-saṁyutam \|***  
***avaiṣṇavānām aprāpyaṁ guṇa-traya-vivarjitam \|\|***  
***nitya-siddhaiḥ samākīrṇaṁ tan-mayaiḥ pāñca-kālikaiḥ \|***  
***sabhā-prāsāda-saṁyuktaṁ vanaiś copavanaiḥ śubham \|\|***  
***vāpī-kūpa-taḍāgaiś ca vṛkṣa-ṣaṇḍaiḥ sumaṇḍitam \|***
